```
1→"""Canonical enums for finding attributes.
     2→
     3→StrEnum values compare equal to their string values (Confidence.HIGH == "high"),
     4→so existing code using raw strings continues to work during gradual migration.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import enum
    10→
    11→
    12→class Confidence(enum.StrEnum):
    13→    HIGH = "high"
    14→    MEDIUM = "medium"
    15→    LOW = "low"
    16→
    17→
    18→class Status(enum.StrEnum):
    19→    OPEN = "open"
    20→    FIXED = "fixed"
    21→    WONTFIX = "wontfix"
    22→    FALSE_POSITIVE = "false_positive"
    23→    AUTO_RESOLVED = "auto_resolved"
    24→    RESOLVED = "resolved"  # Legacy on-disk value; migrated to FIXED on load.
    25→
    26→
    27→_CANONICAL_FINDING_STATUSES = frozenset(
    28→    {
    29→        Status.OPEN.value,
    30→        Status.FIXED.value,
    31→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    32→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    33→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    34→    }
    35→)
    36→_RESOLVED_STATUSES = frozenset(
    37→    {
    38→        Status.FIXED.value,
    39→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    40→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    41→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    42→    }
    43→)
    44→_LEGACY_STATUS_ALIASES = {
    45→    Status.RESOLVED.value: Status.FIXED.value,
    46→}
    47→
    48→
    49→class Tier(enum.IntEnum):
    50→    AUTO_FIX = 1
    51→    QUICK_FIX = 2
    52→    JUDGMENT = 3
    53→    MAJOR_REFACTOR = 4
    54→
    55→
    56→def canonical_finding_status(value: object, *, default: str = Status.OPEN.value) -> str:
    57→    """Normalize legacy/unknown finding status values to a canonical token."""
    58→    token = str(value).strip().lower()
    59→    [REDACTED](token, token)
    60→    return token if token in _CANONICAL_FINDING_STATUSES else default
    61→
    62→
    63→def finding_status_tokens(*, include_all: bool = False) -> frozenset[str]:
    64→    """Return canonical finding-status tokens, optionally including `all`."""
    65→    if include_all:
    66→        return frozenset({*_CANONICAL_FINDING_STATUSES, "all"})
    67→    return _CANONICAL_FINDING_STATUSES
    68→
    69→
    70→def resolved_statuses() -> frozenset[str]:
    71→    """Return the set of statuses that mean a finding is no longer open."""
    72→    return _RESOLVED_STATUSES
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = [
    76→    "Confidence",
    77→    "Status",
    78→    "Tier",
    79→    "canonical_finding_status",
    80→    "finding_status_tokens",
    81→    "resolved_statuses",
    82→]
    83→
```

```
1→"""Section render helpers for markdown planning output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→
     8→def summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
     9→    open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    10→    total_findings = sum(
    11→        stats.get(key, 0) for key in ("open", "fixed", "wontfix", "auto_resolved")
    12→    )
    13→    addressed = total_findings - open_count
    14→    pct = round(addressed / total_findings * 100) if total_findings else 100
    15→    return [
    16→        f"- **{open_count} open** / {total_findings} total ({pct}% addressed)",
    17→        "",
    18→    ]
    19→
    20→
    21→def addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
    22→    addressed = [finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"]
    23→    if not addressed:
    24→        return []
    25→
    26→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
    27→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    28→    for finding in addressed:
    29→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
    30→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
    31→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
    32→
    33→    wontfix = [
    34→        finding
    35→        for finding in addressed
    36→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
    37→    ]
    38→    if wontfix:
    39→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
    40→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
    41→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
    42→    lines.append("")
    43→    return lines
    44→
    45→
    46→def render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
    47→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
    48→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    49→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    50→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    51→
    52→    lines = [f"- [ ] [{confidence}] {summary}"]
    53→    description = override.get("description")
    54→    if description:
    55→        lines.append(f"      → {description}")
    56→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
    57→    note = override.get("note")
    58→    if note:
    59→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
    60→    return lines
    61→
    62→
    63→def plan_user_ordered_section(
    64→    items: list[dict],
    65→    plan: dict,
    66→) -> list[str]:
    67→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
    68→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    71→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    72→
    73→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
    74→    if not ordered_ids:
    75→        return []
    76→
    77→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
    78→    lines: list[str] = [
    79→        "---",
    80→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
    81→        "",
    82→    ]
    83→
    84→    emitted: set[str] = set()
    85→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
    86→        member_ids = [
    87→            finding_id
    88→            for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    89→            if finding_id in ordered_ids and finding_id in by_id
    90→        ]
    91→        if not member_ids:
    92→            continue
    93→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    94→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
    95→        if desc:
    96→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
    97→        lines.append("")
    98→        for finding_id in member_ids:
    99→            item = by_id.get(finding_id)
   100→            if item:
   101→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(finding_id, {})))
   102→                emitted.add(finding_id)
   103→        lines.append("")
   104→
   105→    unclustered = [
   106→        finding_id
   107→        for finding_id in queue_order
   108→        if finding_id in ordered_ids and finding_id not in emitted and finding_id in by_id
   109→    ]
   110→    if unclustered:
   111→        if any(cluster.get("finding_ids") for cluster in clusters.values()):
   112→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   113→            lines.append("")
   114→        for finding_id in unclustered:
   115→            item = by_id.get(finding_id)
   116→            if item:
   117→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(finding_id, {})))
   118→        lines.append("")
   119→    return lines
   120→
   121→
   122→def plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   123→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   124→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   125→    if not skipped:
   126→        return []
   127→
   128→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   129→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   130→
   131→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {"temporary": [], "permanent": [], "false_positive": []}
   132→    for finding_id, entry in skipped.items():
   133→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   134→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(finding_id)
   135→
   136→    kind_labels = {
   137→        "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
   138→        "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
   139→        "false_positive": "False Positives",
   140→    }
   141→
   142→    lines: list[str] = [
   143→        "---",
   144→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   145→        "",
   146→    ]
   147→
   148→    for kind in ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"):
   149→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   150→        if not ids:
   151→            continue
   152→        lines.append(f"### {kind_labels[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   153→        lines.append("")
   154→        for finding_id in ids:
   155→            entry = skipped.get(finding_id, {})
   156→            item = by_id.get(finding_id)
   157→            if item:
   158→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(finding_id, {})))
   159→            else:
   160→                lines.append(f"- ~~{finding_id}~~ (not in current queue)")
   161→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   162→            if reason:
   163→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   164→            note = entry.get("note")
   165→            if note and not overrides.get(finding_id, {}).get("note"):
   166→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   167→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   168→            if review_after:
   169→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   170→                lines.append(f"      Review after: scan {skipped_at + review_after}")
   171→        lines.append("")
   172→    return lines
   173→
   174→
   175→def plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   176→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   177→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   178→    if not superseded:
   179→        return []
   180→
   181→    lines: list[str] = [
   182→        "---",
   183→        f"## Superseded ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   184→        "",
   185→    ]
   186→    for finding_id, entry in superseded.items():
   187→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   188→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   189→        lines.append(f"- ~~{finding_id}~~{summary_str}")
   190→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   191→        if candidates:
   192→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   193→        note = entry.get("note")
   194→        if note:
   195→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   196→    lines.append("")
   197→    return lines
   198→
   199→
```

```
   213→    total_count = len(open_items)
   214→    if not open_items:
   215→        return lines
   216→
   217→    lines.extend([
   218→        "---",
   219→        f"## Open Items ({total_count})",
   220→        "",
   221→    ])
   222→
   223→    sorted_files = sorted(
   224→        by_file.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→    )
   226→    for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→        lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→        lines.append("")
   230→        for item in file_items:
   231→            if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                    lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                continue
   237→
   238→            conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→            lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→            lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→        lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   247→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   248→
   249→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   250→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   251→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   252→    """
   253→    findings = state["findings"]
   254→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   255→
   256→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   257→    if plan is None:
   258→        try:
   259→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   260→            plan = load_plan()
   261→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   262→            plan = {}
   263→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   264→        plan = {}
   265→    plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   266→    plan.setdefault("skipped", {})
   267→    plan.setdefault("clusters", {})
   268→
   269→    has_plan = bool(
   270→        plan
   271→        and (
   272→            plan.get("queue_order")
   273→            or plan.get("skipped")
   274→            or plan.get("clusters")
   275→        )
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   279→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   280→    lines.extend(_summary_lines(stats))
   281→
   282→    if has_plan:
   283→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   284→        queue = build_work_queue(
   285→            state,
   286→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   287→                count=None,
   288→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   289→                status="open",
   290→                include_subjective=True,
   291→            ),
   292→        )
   293→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   294→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   295→
   296→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   297→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   298→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   299→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   300→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   301→        if remaining:
   302→            lines.append("---")
   303→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   304→            lines.append("")
   305→
   306→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   307→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   308→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   309→    else:
   310→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   311→
   312→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   313→
   314→    return "\n".join(lines)
   315→
```

```
   266→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.

   280→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   281→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   282→            skipped.pop(fid)
   283→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   284→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   285→            resurfaced.append(fid)
```

```
   551→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   552→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   553→
   554→    Returns True when any ``triage::*`` stage ID is in the queue OR
   555→    genuinely *new* review findings appeared since the last triage.
   556→
   557→    When findings are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   558→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   559→    through the plan.
   560→    """
   561→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   562→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   563→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
```

Looking at queue_render.py lines 156-168 again, I see it ALWAYS passes `status="open"`. So the filtering should work.

Unless... wait. Let me check if there's something about how `plan_data` affects the filtering. Let me look at `_resolve_plan_context` on line 21 of queue_render.py:

```python
def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    plan_data: dict | None = None
    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
        plan_data = plan

    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
        if active_cluster:
            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    return plan_data, effective_cluster
```

So if queue_order is empty AND there are no overrides/clusters, then plan_data is None. And then it gets passed as None to build_work_queue.

Now let me check - when plan_data is None, does it affect the filtering? Let me look at build_work_queue again (core.py line 64-183):

When `plan=None` is passed, it doesn't add triage stages or workflow items. But the key filtering still happens in `build_finding_items` which filters by status="open".

So the bug must be elsewhere. Wait - let me re-read the issue description again carefully. It says "But it includes findings with `status: "wontfix"` — these should be filtered out."

```
    61→def _state_with_findings(*ids: str) -> dict:
    62→    findings = {}
    63→    for fid in ids:
    64→        findings[fid] = {
    65→            "id": fid,
    66→            "status": "open",
    67→            "detector": "unused",
    68→            "file": "test.py",
    69→            "tier": 1,
    70→            "confidence": "high",
    71→            "summary": f"Finding {fid}",
    72→        }
    73→    return {"findings": findings, "scan_count": 5}
    74→
    75→
    76→def _setup_plan(tmp_path, monkeypatch, queue_order: list[str], clusters: dict | None = None):
    77→    """Create and save a plan with given queue order, monkeypatch PLAN_FILE."""
    78→    import desloppify.engine._plan.persistence as persist_mod
    79→
    80→    plan_file = tmp_path / "plan.json"
    81→    plan = empty_plan()
    82→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    83→    plan["queue_order"] = queue_order
    84→    if clusters:
    85→        plan["clusters"] = clusters
    86→    save_plan(plan, plan_file)
    87→    monkeypatch.setattr(persist_mod, "PLAN_FILE", plan_file)
    88→
```

```
1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     8→
     9→
    10→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    status: str,
    14→) -> bool:
    15→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    16→    if status != "fixed":
    17→        return False
    18→    try:
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return False
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    23→        if not queue_order:
    24→            return False
    25→
    26→        result = build_work_queue(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
    29→                plan=plan,
    30→                count=1,
    31→                collapse_clusters=True,
    32→                include_subjective=True,
    33→            ),
    34→        )
    35→        if not result["items"]:
    36→            return False
    37→
    38→        front_item = result["items"][0]
    39→        front_id = front_item["id"]
    40→
    41→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
    42→        if front_item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    43→            for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    44→                front_ids.add(member["id"])
    45→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    46→        else:
    47→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    48→
    49→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    50→        resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    51→        for pattern in patterns:
    52→            if pattern in clusters:
    53→                resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", []))
    54→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    55→            else:
    56→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    57→
    58→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    59→        resolved_ids = {
    60→            finding_id
    61→            for finding_id in resolved_ids
    62→            if finding_id in clusters
    63→            or (finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open")
    64→        }
    65→        if not resolved_ids:
    66→            return False
    67→
    68→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
    69→        for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    70→            if cluster_id in clusters:
    71→                alive_members = {
    72→                    finding_id
    73→                    for finding_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("finding_ids", [])
    74→                    if finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open"
    75→                }
    76→                if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    77→                    out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    78→        if not out_of_order:
    79→            return False
    80→
    81→        print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    82→        for finding_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    83→            print(f"    {finding_id}")
    84→        print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    85→        print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    86→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan move <pattern> --position top    # move to front", "dim"))
    87→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    89→        return True
    90→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    91→        return False
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
    95→
```

```
   263→def resurface_stale_skips(
```

Now let me compile my complete findings:

## Summary of Investigation

I've traced the full code path for the `desloppify plan queue` command. Here's what I found:

### Code Flow:
1. **Entry point** (`queue_render.py:141-170`): `cmd_plan_queue` calls `build_work_queue` with `status="open"`
2. **Queue building** (`core.py:64-183`): `build_work_queue` calls `build_finding_items` with the status filter
3. **Filtering** (`ranking.py:92-150`): `build_finding_items` filters findings using `status_matches` which checks if `finding.get("status") == "open"`
4. **Status matching** (`helpers.py:33-34`): Simple equality check - only includes findings matching the status filter

### Plan Context Resolution:
- (`queue_render.py:21-31`): `_resolve_plan_context` determines if plan data should be used
  - Sets `plan_data = plan` only if `plan.get("queue_order")` OR `plan.get("overrides")` OR `plan.get("clusters")`
  - Note: Does NOT check for `skipped` entries

### Key Finding - The Root Cause:
When `queue_order` is empty (`[]`), the question is: **Are there overrides or clusters present?**

If the plan HAS overrides or clusters (even with empty queue_order), then `plan_data` is NOT None, and `build_work_queue` receives the plan. However, even in this case, the filtering should work correctly because:

1. `build_finding_items` still filters by `status="open"` (line 107 in ranking.py)
2. Findings with status="wontfix" are excluded from the items list
3. `apply_plan_order` only works with items that are in the filtered list

### Potential Issue Scenarios:

**Scenario 1**: If findings are in `plan["skipped"]` dict with `kind="permanent"` (wontfix), but the corresponding findings have status="wontfix" in state:
- When `include_skipped=False` (default), they should NOT appear
- When `include_skipped=True`, they WOULD appear but marked with `plan_skipped=True`

**Scenario 2**: If there's a race condition where:
- A finding is marked as wontfix in the state
- But reconciliation hasn't run yet to update plan references
- And the finding is still in queue_order

**Scenario 3**: Edge case in reconciliation where wontfix findings aren't being properly superseded or removed from queue_order

### Files Involved in the Issue:
- **Command entry**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py` (lines 21-31, 141-170)
- **Queue building**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` (lines 64-183)
- **Finding filtering**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` (lines 92-150)
- **Status matching**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` (lines 33-34)
- **Plan ordering**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py` (lines 23-120)
- **Plan reconciliation**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py` (lines 132-217)
- **Skip operations**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py` (lines 204-286)

The bug likely manifests when findings with status="wontfix" remain in the queue_order after the plan state gets out of sync with the actual finding states.

```
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    13→    ALL_STATUSES,
    14→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    15→    build_create_plan_item,
    16→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    17→    build_subjective_items,
    18→    build_triage_stage_items,
    19→    scope_matches,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    22→    build_finding_items,
    23→    enrich_with_impact,
    24→    group_queue_items,
    25→    item_explain,
    26→    item_sort_key,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    29→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    30→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    31→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    34→
    35→
    36→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    37→class QueueBuildOptions:
    38→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    39→
    40→    count: int | None = 1
    41→    scan_path: str | None = None
    42→    scope: str | None = None
    43→    status: str = "open"
    44→    include_subjective: bool = True
    45→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    46→    chronic: bool = False
    47→    explain: bool = False
    48→    plan: dict | None = None
    49→    include_skipped: bool = False
    50→    cluster: str | None = None
    51→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    52→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
    53→
    54→
    55→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    56→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    57→
    58→    items: list[dict]
    59→    total: int
    60→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    61→    new_ids: set[str]
    62→
    63→
    64→def build_work_queue(
    65→    state: StateModel,
    66→    *,
    67→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    68→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    69→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    70→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    71→
    72→    status = resolved_options.status
    73→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    74→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    75→    try:
    76→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    77→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    78→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    79→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    80→
    81→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    82→        state,
    83→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    84→        status_filter=status,
    85→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    86→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    87→    )
    88→
    89→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    90→
    91→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating
    92→    policy = resolved_options.policy or compute_subjective_visibility(state)
    93→
    94→    if (
    95→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    96→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    97→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    98→    ):
    99→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   100→            state,
   101→            state.get("findings", {}),
   102→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   103→        )
   104→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   105→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   106→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   107→            set(resolved_options.plan.get("queue_order", []))
   108→            if resolved_options.plan
   109→            else set()
   110→        )
   111→        for item in subjective_items:
   112→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   113→                continue
   114→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   115→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   116→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   117→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   118→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   119→                continue
   120→            all_items.append(item)
   121→
   122→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   123→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   124→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   125→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   126→        checkpoint_item = build_score_checkpoint_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   127→        if checkpoint_item is not None:
   128→            all_items.append(checkpoint_item)
   129→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   130→        if plan_item is not None:
   131→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   132→
   133→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   134→
   135→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   136→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   137→    all_items = [
   138→        item for item in all_items
   139→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   140→        or item.get("is_review")
   141→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   142→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   143→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   144→    ]
   145→
   146→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   147→
   148→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   149→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   150→    if resolved_options.plan:
   151→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   152→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   153→            all_items,
   154→            resolved_options.plan,
   155→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   156→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   157→            new_ids=new_ids,
   158→        )
   159→
   160→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   161→    should_collapse = (
   162→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   163→        and resolved_options.plan
   164→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   165→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   166→    )
   167→    if should_collapse:
   168→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   169→
   170→    total = len(all_items)
   171→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   172→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   173→
   174→    if resolved_options.explain:
   175→        for item in all_items:
   176→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   177→
   178→    return {
   179→        "items": all_items,
   180→        "total": total,
   181→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   182→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   183→    }
   184→
   185→
   186→__all__ = [
   187→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   188→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   189→    "WorkQueueResult",
   190→    "build_work_queue",
   191→    "group_queue_items",
   192→]
   193→
```

```
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))

    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (

    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    52→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    53→        return True
    54→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    55→        members = item.get("members", [])
    56→        return bool(members) and all(
    57→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    58→        )
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    63→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    64→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    65→    weight_by_confidence = {
    66→        "high": 1.0,
    67→        "medium": 0.7,
    68→        "low": 0.3,
    69→    }
    70→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    71→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    72→        weight *= 10.0
    73→    return float(weight)
    74→
    75→
    76→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    77→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    78→    if not scope:
    79→        return True
    80→
    81→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    82→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    83→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    84→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    85→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    86→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    87→
    88→    if "*" in scope:
    89→        return any(
    90→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    91→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    92→        )
    93→
    94→    if "::" in scope:
    95→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    96→
    97→    lowered = scope.lower()
    98→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        return (
   100→            lowered in item_id.lower()
   101→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
   102→            or lowered in summary.lower()
   103→        )
   104→
   105→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   106→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   107→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   108→
   109→    return (
   110→        detector == scope
   111→        or filepath == scope
   112→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   113→    )
   114→

   116→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   117→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   118→
   119→
   120→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   121→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   122→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   123→    target = cleaned.lower()
   124→
   125→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   126→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   127→            return str(dim_key)
   128→    return slugify(cleaned)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   132→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   133→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   134→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   135→    return {
   136→        cleaned.lower(),
   137→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   138→        slugify(cleaned),
   139→        canonical.lower(),
   140→        slugify(canonical),
   141→    }

   144→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   145→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   146→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   147→    if not lang:
   148→        return None
   149→
   150→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   151→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   155→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   156→        return None
   157→
   158→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   159→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   160→        return None
   161→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   162→
   163→
   164→def primary_command_for_finding(
   165→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   166→) -> str:
   167→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   168→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   169→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   170→        available_fixers = [
   171→            fixer
   172→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   173→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   174→        ]
   175→        if available_fixers:
   176→            return f"desloppify autofix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   177→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   178→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   179→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   180→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   181→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'

   184→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   186→
   187→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   188→    """
   189→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   190→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   191→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   192→    )
   193→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   194→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   195→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   199→    order_set = set(order)
   200→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   201→    if not present:
   202→        return []
   203→
   204→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   205→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   206→
   207→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   208→    open_review_count = sum(
   209→        1 for f in findings.values()
   210→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   211→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   212→    )
   213→
   214→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   215→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   216→
   217→    items: list[dict] = []
   218→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   219→        if sid not in present:
   220→            continue
   221→        if name in confirmed:
   222→            continue
   223→
   224→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   225→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   226→        blocked_by = sorted(
   227→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   228→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   232→        if name == "commit":
   233→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   234→
   235→        items.append({
   236→            "id": sid,
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "confidence": "high",
   239→            "detector": "triage",
   240→            "file": ".",
   241→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   242→            "stage_name": name,
   243→            "stage_index": idx,
   244→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   247→                "stage": name,
   248→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   249→            },
   250→            "primary_command": cmd,
   251→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   252→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   253→        })
   254→    return items

   399→            str(key).strip().lower()
   400→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   401→            if str(key).strip()
   402→        ]
   403→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   404→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   405→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   406→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   407→            name in unassessed_dims
   408→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   409→        )
   410→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   411→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   412→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   413→        if open_review > 0:
   414→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   415→        else:
   416→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   417→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   418→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   419→        items.append(
   420→            {
   421→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   422→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   423→                "file": ".",
   424→                "confidence": "medium",
   425→                "summary": summary,
   426→                "detail": {
   427→                    "dimension_name": name,
   428→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   429→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   430→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   431→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   432→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   433→                },
   434→                "status": "open",
   435→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   436→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   437→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   438→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   439→            }
   440→        )
   441→    return items
   442→
   443→
   444→__all__ = [
   445→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   446→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   447→    "build_create_plan_item",
   448→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   449→    "build_subjective_items",
   450→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   451→    "detail_dict",
   452→    "is_review_finding",
   453→    "is_subjective_finding",
   454→    "is_subjective_queue_item",
   455→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   456→    "review_finding_weight",
   457→    "scope_matches",
   458→    "slugify",
   459→    "status_matches",
   460→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   461→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   462→]
   463→
```

```
1→"""State-backed work queue for review findings.
     2→
     3→Review findings live in state["findings"]. This module provides:
     4→- Listing/sorting open review findings by impact
     5→- Storing investigation notes on findings
     6→- Expiring stale holistic findings during scan
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    13→
    14→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import detail_dict
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→__all__ = [
    20→    "impact_label",
    21→    "list_open_review_findings",
    22→    "update_investigation",
    23→    "expire_stale_holistic",
    24→]
    25→
    26→
    27→def impact_label(weight: float) -> str:
    28→    """Convert weight to a human-readable impact label."""
    29→    try:
    30→        numeric = float(weight)
    31→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    32→        return "+"
    33→    if numeric >= 8:
    34→        return "+++"
    35→    if numeric >= 5:
    36→        return "++"
    37→    return "+"
    38→
    39→
    40→def list_open_review_findings(state: dict) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return open review findings sorted by impact (highest first)."""
    42→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    43→    review = [
    44→        finding
    45→        for finding in findings.values()
    46→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
    47→    ]
    48→
    49→    def _sort_key(finding: dict) -> tuple[float, str]:
    50→        weight, _impact, finding_id = finding_weight(finding)
    51→        return (-weight, finding_id)
    52→
    53→    review.sort(key=_sort_key)
    54→    return review
    55→
    56→
    57→def update_investigation(state: dict, finding_id: str, text: str) -> bool:
    58→    """Store investigation text on a finding. Returns False if not found/not open."""
    59→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    60→    if not finding or finding.get("status") != "open":
    61→        return False
    62→    detail = detail_dict(finding)
    63→    if not detail:
    64→        detail = {}
    65→        finding["detail"] = detail
    66→    detail["investigation"] = text
    67→    detail["investigated_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
    68→    return True
    69→
    70→
    71→def expire_stale_holistic(state: dict, max_age_days: int = 30) -> list[str]:
    72→    """Auto-resolve holistic review findings older than max_age_days."""
    73→    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    74→    expired: list[str] = []
    75→
    76→    for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    77→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    78→            continue
    79→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    80→            continue
    81→        if not detail_dict(finding).get("holistic"):
    82→            continue
    83→
    84→        last_seen = finding.get("last_seen")
    85→        if not last_seen:
    86→            continue
    87→
    88→        try:
    89→            seen_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_seen)
    90→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    91→            logger.debug(
    92→                "Skipping holistic finding %s with invalid last_seen %r: %s",
    93→                finding_id,
    94→                last_seen,
    95→                exc,
    96→            )
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        age_days = (now - seen_dt).days
   100→        if age_days > max_age_days:
   101→            finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   102→            finding["resolved_at"] = now.isoformat()
   103→            finding["note"] = "holistic review expired — re-run review to re-evaluate"
   104→            expired.append(finding_id)
   105→
   106→    return expired
   107→
```

```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     6→
     7→
     8→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
     9→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    10→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    11→    if not scan_history:
    12→        return set()
    13→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    14→    if not threshold:
    15→        return set()
    16→    return {
    17→        finding_id
    18→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    19→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    20→    }
    21→
    22→
    23→def apply_plan_order(
    24→    items: list[dict],
    25→    plan: dict,
    26→    *,
    27→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    28→    cluster: str | None = None,
    29→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    30→) -> list[dict]:
    31→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    32→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    33→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    35→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    36→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    37→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→
    39→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    40→    for item in items:
    41→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    42→
    43→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    44→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    45→        if override.get("description"):
    46→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    47→        if override.get("note"):
    48→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    49→        if override.get("cluster"):
    50→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    51→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    52→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    53→                "name": cluster_name,
    54→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    55→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    56→            }
    57→
    58→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    59→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    60→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    61→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    62→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    63→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    64→
    65→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    66→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    67→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    68→    _new = new_ids or set()
    69→    for item in items:
    70→        item_id = item["id"]
    71→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    72→            continue
    73→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    74→            skipped_items.append(item)
    75→        elif item_id in _new:
    76→            remaining_new.append(item)
    77→        else:
    78→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    79→
    80→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    81→
    82→    # Triage stage IDs can appear in queue_order, so plan ordering may
    83→    # scramble their dependency order.  Re-sort just the stage items
    84→    # in-place to restore unblocked-before-blocked invariant.
    85→    stage_entries = [
    86→        (i, item) for i, item in enumerate(result)
    87→        if item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    88→    ]
    89→    if len(stage_entries) > 1:
    90→        sorted_stages = sorted(
    91→            [item for _, item in stage_entries],
    92→            key=lambda it: (
    93→                1 if it.get("is_blocked") else 0,
    94→                int(it.get("stage_index", 0)),
    95→            ),
    96→        )
    97→        for (idx, _), replacement in zip(stage_entries, sorted_stages):
    98→            result[idx] = replacement
    99→
   100→    if include_skipped:
   101→        result = result + skipped_items
   102→
   103→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
   104→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   105→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   106→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   107→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   108→            if skip_entry:
   109→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   110→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   111→                if skip_reason:
   112→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   113→
   114→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   115→    if effective_cluster:
   116→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   117→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   118→        if cluster_member_ids:
   119→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   120→    return result
   121→
   122→
   123→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   124→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   125→    try:
   126→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   127→
   128→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   129→        if meta:
   130→            return meta.action_type
   131→    except ImportError as exc:
   132→        _ = exc
   133→    return "manual_fix"
   134→
   135→
   136→def _build_cluster_meta(
   137→    cluster_name: str, members: list[dict], cluster_data: dict
   138→) -> dict:
   139→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   140→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   141→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   142→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   143→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   144→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   145→        action_type = "reorganize"
   146→    else:
   147→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   148→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   149→            action_type = "refactor"
   150→
   151→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   152→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   153→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   154→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   155→    else:
   156→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   157→
   158→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   159→    if not primary_command:
   160→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   161→
   162→    estimated_impact = max(
   163→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    return {
   167→        "id": cluster_name,
   168→        "kind": "cluster",
   169→        "action_type": action_type,
   170→        "summary": summary,
   171→        "members": members,
   172→        "member_count": len(members),
   173→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   174→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   175→        "cluster_auto": True,
   176→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   177→        "confidence": "high",
   178→        "detector": detector,
   179→        "file": "",
   180→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   181→    }
   182→
   183→
   184→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   186→
   187→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   188→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   189→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   190→    """
   191→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   192→    if not clusters:
   193→        return items
   194→
   195→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   196→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   197→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   198→            continue
   199→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   200→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   201→
   202→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   203→        return items
   204→
   205→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   206→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   207→    for item in items:
   208→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   209→        if cname:
   210→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   211→
   212→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   213→    meta_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
   214→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   215→        if len(members) < 2:
   216→            continue
   217→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   218→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   219→        )
   220→
   221→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   222→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   223→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   224→    result: list[dict] = []
   225→    for item in items:
   226→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   227→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   228→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   229→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   230→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   231→            # skip individual member
   232→        else:
   233→            result.append(item)
   234→    return result
   235→
   236→
   237→__all__ = [
   238→    "apply_plan_order",
   239→    "collapse_clusters",
   240→    "new_item_ids",
   241→]
   242→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    19→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    20→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    21→
    22→
    23→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    25→
    26→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    27→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    28→    """
    29→    if not dimension_scores:
    30→        for item in items:
    31→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    35→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    36→
    37→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    38→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    39→
    40→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    41→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    42→    for entry in entries:
    43→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    44→        if not name:
    45→            continue
    46→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    47→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    48→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    49→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    50→
    51→    for item in items:
    52→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    53→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    54→
    55→
    56→def _compute_item_impact(
    57→    item: dict,
    58→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    59→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    60→) -> float:
    61→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    62→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    63→
    64→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    65→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    66→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    67→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    68→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    69→        if entry:
    70→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    71→        return 0.0
    72→
    73→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    74→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    75→    if detector:
    76→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    77→        if dimension:
    78→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    79→            if entry:
    80→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    81→
    82→    return 0.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    86→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    88→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    89→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    90→
    91→
    92→def build_finding_items(
    93→    state: dict,
    94→    *,
    95→    scan_path: str | None,
    96→    status_filter: str,
    97→    scope: str | None,
    98→    chronic: bool,
    99→) -> list[dict]:
   100→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
   101→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   102→    out: list[dict] = []
   103→
   104→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   105→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   106→            continue
   107→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   108→            continue
   109→        if chronic and not (
   110→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   111→        ):
   112→            continue
   113→
   114→        # Evidence-only: skip findings below standalone confidence threshold
   115→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   116→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   117→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   118→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   119→            finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   120→            if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
   121→                continue
   122→
   123→        item = dict(finding)
   124→        item["id"] = finding_id
   125→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   126→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   127→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   128→        item["review_weight"] = (
   129→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   130→        )
   131→        subjective_score = None
   132→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   133→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   134→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   135→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   136→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   137→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   138→            )
   139→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   140→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   141→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   142→            item,
   143→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   144→        )
   145→
   146→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   147→            continue
   148→        out.append(item)
   149→
   150→    return out
   151→
   152→
   153→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   154→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   155→
   156→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
   157→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   158→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   159→
   160→    # Triage stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
   161→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   162→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   163→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   164→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   165→
   166→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
   167→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   168→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   169→
   170→    if kind == "cluster":
   171→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   172→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   173→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   174→        )
   175→        return (
   176→            0,
   177→            action_pri,
   178→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   179→            item.get("id", ""),
   180→        )
   181→
   182→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   183→
   184→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   185→        return (
   186→            1,
   187→            -impact,
   188→            subjective_score_value(item),
   189→            item.get("id", ""),
   190→        )
   191→
   192→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   193→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   194→    return (
   195→        1,
   196→        -impact,
   197→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   198→        -review_weight,
   199→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   200→        item.get("id", ""),
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   205→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   206→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   207→        return {
   208→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   209→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   210→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   211→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   212→            "policy": "Triage stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   213→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   214→        }
   215→
   216→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   217→        return {
   218→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   219→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after triage stages, before findings.",
   220→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   221→        }
   222→
   223→    if kind == "cluster":
   224→        return {
   225→            "kind": "cluster",
   226→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   227→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
   228→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", 0),
   229→            "policy": "Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type then size.",
   230→            "ranking_factors": ["action_type asc", "member_count desc", "id asc"],
   231→        }
   232→
   233→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   234→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   235→        return {
   236→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   237→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   238→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   239→            "initial_review": initial,
   240→            "policy": (
   241→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   242→                if initial else
   243→                "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   244→            ),
   245→            "ranking_factors": ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   246→        }
   247→
   248→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   249→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   250→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   251→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   252→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   253→    if is_subjective:
   254→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   255→    elif is_review:
   256→        ranking_factors = [
   257→            "estimated_impact desc",
   258→            "confidence asc",
   259→            "review_weight desc",
   260→            "count desc",
   261→            "id asc",
   262→        ]
   263→    else:
   264→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   265→    explain = {
   266→        "kind": "finding",
   267→        "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   268→        "confidence": confidence,
   269→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   270→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   271→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   272→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   273→    }
   274→    if is_review:
   275→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   276→    if is_subjective:
   277→        explain["policy"] = (
   278→            "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   279→        )
   280→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   281→    return explain
   282→
   283→
   284→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   285→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   286→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   287→    for item in items:
   288→        if group == "file":
   289→            key = item.get("file", "")
   290→        elif group == "detector":
   291→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   292→        elif group == "cluster":
   293→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   294→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   295→        else:
   296→            key = "items"
   297→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   298→    return grouped
   299→
   300→
   301→__all__ = [
   302→    "build_finding_items",
   303→    "enrich_with_impact",
   304→    "item_explain",
   305→    "item_sort_key",
   306→    "subjective_score_value",
   307→    "group_queue_items",
   308→]
   309→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
     9→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    10→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    13→    ALL_STATUSES,
    14→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    15→    build_create_plan_item,
    16→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    17→    build_subjective_items,
    18→    build_triage_stage_items,
    19→    scope_matches,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    22→    build_finding_items,
    23→    enrich_with_impact,
    24→    group_queue_items,
    25→    item_explain,
    26→    item_sort_key,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    29→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    30→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    31→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    34→
    35→
    36→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    37→class QueueBuildOptions:
    38→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    39→
    40→    count: int | None = 1
    41→    scan_path: str | None = None
    42→    scope: str | None = None
    43→    status: str = "open"
    44→    include_subjective: bool = True
    45→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    46→    chronic: bool = False
    47→    explain: bool = False
    48→    plan: dict | None = None
    49→    include_skipped: bool = False
    50→    cluster: str | None = None
    51→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    52→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
    53→
    54→
    55→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    56→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    57→
    58→    items: list[dict]
    59→    total: int
    60→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    61→    new_ids: set[str]
    62→
    63→
    64→def build_work_queue(
    65→    state: StateModel,
    66→    *,
    67→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    68→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    69→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    70→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    71→
    72→    status = resolved_options.status
    73→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    74→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    75→    try:
    76→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    77→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    78→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    79→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    80→
    81→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    82→        state,
    83→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    84→        status_filter=status,
    85→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    86→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    87→    )
    88→
    89→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    90→
    91→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating
    92→    policy = resolved_options.policy or compute_subjective_visibility(state)
    93→
    94→    if (
    95→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    96→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    97→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    98→    ):
    99→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   100→            state,
   101→            state.get("findings", {}),
   102→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   103→        )
   104→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   105→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   106→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   107→            set(resolved_options.plan.get("queue_order", []))
   108→            if resolved_options.plan
   109→            else set()
   110→        )
   111→        for item in subjective_items:
   112→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   113→                continue
   114→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   115→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   116→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   117→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   118→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   119→                continue
   120→            all_items.append(item)
   121→
   122→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   123→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   124→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   125→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   126→        checkpoint_item = build_score_checkpoint_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   127→        if checkpoint_item is not None:
   128→            all_items.append(checkpoint_item)
   129→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   130→        if plan_item is not None:
   131→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   132→
   133→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   134→
   135→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   136→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   137→    all_items = [
   138→        item for item in all_items
   139→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   140→        or item.get("is_review")
   141→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   142→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   143→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   144→    ]
   145→
   146→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   147→
   148→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   149→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   150→    if resolved_options.plan:
   151→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   152→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   153→            all_items,
   154→            resolved_options.plan,
   155→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   156→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   157→            new_ids=new_ids,
   158→        )
   159→
   160→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   161→    should_collapse = (
   162→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   163→        and resolved_options.plan
   164→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   165→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   166→    )
   167→    if should_collapse:
   168→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   169→
   170→    total = len(all_items)
   171→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   172→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   173→
   174→    if resolved_options.explain:
   175→        for item in all_items:
   176→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   177→
   178→    return {
   179→        "items": all_items,
   180→        "total": total,
   181→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   182→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   183→    }
   184→
   185→
   186→__all__ = [
   187→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   188→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   189→    "WorkQueueResult",
   190→    "build_work_queue",
   191→    "group_queue_items",
   192→]
   193→
```
