    31→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    32→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    33→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    34→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    35→from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
    36→
    37→
    38→def _scorecard_subjective(
    39→    state: dict,
    40→    dim_scores: dict,
    41→) -> list[dict]:
    42→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    43→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    56→            continue
    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    58→        if strict_val < threshold:
    59→            low.append(
    60→                (
    61→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low
    68→
    69→
    70→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    72→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    73→    state = runtime.state
    74→    config = runtime.config
    75→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    79→    if skill_warning:
    80→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    81→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    82→    if config_warning:
    83→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    84→
    85→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    86→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    90→    plan_data: dict | None,
    91→    *,
    92→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    93→    scope: str | None,
    94→) -> str | None:
    95→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    96→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    97→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    98→        if active_cluster:
    99→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   100→    return effective_cluster
   101→
   102→
   103→def _build_next_payload(
   104→    *,
   105→    queue: dict,
   106→    items: list[dict],
   107→    state: dict,
   108→    narrative: dict,
   109→    plan_data: dict | None,
   110→) -> dict:
   111→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   112→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   113→    )
   114→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   115→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   116→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   117→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   118→        state,
   119→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   120→    )
   121→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   122→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   123→    ]
   124→    return payload
   125→
   126→
   127→def _emit_requested_output(
   128→    args,
   129→    payload: dict,
   130→    items: list[dict],
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   133→    if output_file:
   134→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   135→            output_file,
   136→            payload,
   137→            len(items),
   138→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   139→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   140→        ):
   141→            return True
   142→        raise SystemExit(1)
   143→
   144→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   145→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   146→        return True
   147→    return False
   148→
   149→
   150→def _plan_queue_context(
   151→    *,
   152→    state: dict,
   153→    plan_data: dict | None,
   154→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   155→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan_data)
   156→    try:
   157→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data)
   158→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   159→        breakdown = None
   160→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   161→
   162→
   163→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   164→    try:
   165→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   166→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   167→        return raw_potentials or None
   168→
   169→
   170→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   171→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   172→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   173→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   174→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   175→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   176→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   177→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   178→
   179→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   180→
   181→    # Load the living plan
   182→    plan = load_plan()
   183→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   184→    if (
   185→        plan.get("queue_order")
   186→        or plan.get("overrides")
   187→        or plan.get("clusters")
   188→    ):
   189→        plan_data = plan
   190→
   191→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   192→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   193→        plan_data,
   194→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   195→        scope=scope,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=count,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status,
   205→            include_subjective=True,
   206→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   207→            explain=explain,
   208→            plan=plan_data,
   209→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   210→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   211→        ),
   212→    )
   213→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   214→
   215→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   216→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   217→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   218→        state,
   219→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   220→    )
   221→
   222→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   223→        queue=queue,
   224→        items=items,
   225→        state=state,
   226→        narrative=narrative,
   227→        plan_data=plan_data,
   228→    )
   229→    write_query(payload)
   230→
   231→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   232→        return
   233→
   234→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   235→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   236→        state.get("findings", {}),
   237→        state.get("scan_path"),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   241→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   242→        state=state,
   243→        plan_data=plan_data,
   244→    )
   245→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   246→
   247→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   248→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   249→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   250→        queue,
   251→        strict_score,
   252→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   253→        target_strict=target_strict,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   258→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   259→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   260→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   261→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   262→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   263→    )
   264→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   265→    next_render_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   266→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   267→        state,
   268→        dim_scores,
   269→        findings_scoped,
   270→        strict_score=strict_score,
   271→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   272→        queue_total=queue_total,
   273→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   274→        breakdown=breakdown,
   275→    )
   276→    print()
   277→
   278→
   279→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   280→
```

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
    10→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class ResolvedEntity:
    19→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    20→
    21→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    22→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    23→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    24→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    25→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    29→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    30→        return None
    31→    return ResolvedEntity(
    32→        kind="special_view",
    33→        pattern=pattern,
    34→        display_name=pattern,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    39→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    40→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    41→    if not detectors:
    42→        return None
    43→    dim_display = pattern
    44→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    45→        dim_lowered = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    46→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim_lowered == lowered:
    47→            dim_display = dim.name
    48→            break
    49→    return ResolvedEntity(
    50→        kind="dimension",
    51→        pattern=pattern,
    52→        display_name=dim_display,
    53→        detectors=tuple(detectors),
    54→        is_subjective=False,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→def _resolve_subjective_dimension(
    59→    pattern: str,
    60→    lowered: str,
    61→    state: dict,
    62→) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    63→    display_name = scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    64→    if not display_name:
    65→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    66→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    67→                display_name = key
    68→                break
    69→    if not display_name:
    70→        return None
    71→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    72→    is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    73→        dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    74→    )
    75→    return ResolvedEntity(
    76→        kind="dimension",
    77→        pattern=pattern,
    78→        display_name=display_name,
    79→        detectors=(),
    80→        is_subjective=is_subj,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    85→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    86→
    87→    Resolution priority:
    88→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    89→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    90→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    91→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    92→    """
    93→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    94→
    95→    # 1. Special views
    96→    special_view = _resolve_special_view(pattern, lowered)
    97→    if special_view is not None:
    98→        return special_view
    99→
   100→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
   101→    mechanical = _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern, lowered)
   102→    if mechanical is not None:
   103→        return mechanical
   104→
   105→    # 3. Subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
   106→    subjective = _resolve_subjective_dimension(pattern, lowered, state)
   107→    if subjective is not None:
   108→        return subjective
   109→
   110→    # 4. Everything else
   111→    return ResolvedEntity(
   112→        kind="file_or_pattern",
   113→        pattern=pattern,
   114→        display_name=pattern,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→
   118→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   119→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
   120→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   121→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   122→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   123→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
   124→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
   125→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   126→        if key not in lookup:
   127→            lookup[key] = detectors
   128→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
   129→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   130→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   131→        normalized_display = display.lower()
   132→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
   133→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   134→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
   135→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
   136→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   137→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
   138→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
   139→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
   140→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
   141→    return lookup
   142→
   143→
   144→def _lookup_dimension_score(
   145→    state: dict, display_name: str,
   146→) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   147→    """Find dimension_scores entry with case-insensitive fallback.
   148→
   149→    Returns (dim_data_dict, resolved_display_name).
   150→    """
   151→    lowered = display_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   152→    dim_data = (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(display_name, {})
   153→    if not dim_data:
   154→        for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
   155→            if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
   156→                dim_data = ds_val
   157→                display_name = ds_key
   158→                break
   159→    return dim_data, display_name
   160→
   161→
   162→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
   163→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   164→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   165→    if names:
   166→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   167→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   168→
   169→
   170→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   171→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   172→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   173→    pattern = args.pattern
   174→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   175→    if chronic:
   176→        scope = pattern
   177→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   178→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   179→    if not pattern:
   180→        print(
   181→            colorize(
   182→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   183→                "yellow",
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   187→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   188→
   189→
   190→def load_matches(
   191→    state: dict,
   192→    *,
   193→    scope: str | None,
   194→    status_filter: str,
   195→    chronic: bool,
   196→) -> list[dict]:
   197→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=None,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status_filter,
   205→            include_subjective=False,
   206→            chronic=chronic,
   207→        ),
   208→    )
   209→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   210→
   211→
   212→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict], *, no_budget: bool = False):
   213→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches.
   214→
   215→    When *no_budget* is True, all matches are surfaced (nothing hidden).
   216→    """
   217→    if no_budget:
   218→        return (
   219→            matches,
   220→            {},
   221→            0,
   222→            0,
   223→            None,
   224→        )
   225→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   226→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   227→    )
   228→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   229→        matches,
   230→        budget=noise_budget,
   231→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   232→    )
   233→    return (
   234→        surfaced_matches,
   235→        hidden_by_detector,
   236→        noise_budget,
   237→        global_noise_budget,
   238→        budget_warning,
   239→    )
   240→
   241→
   242→__all__ = [
   243→    "ResolvedEntity",
   244→    "_detector_names_hint",
   245→    "_lookup_dimension_score",
   246→    "load_matches",
   247→    "resolve_entity",
   248→    "resolve_noise",
   249→    "resolve_show_scope",
   250→]
   251→

1→"""Finding prioritization/selection helpers for next items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanItem, PlanState
     6→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def get_next_items(
    13→    state: PlanState,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int = 1,
    16→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    17→) -> list[PlanItem]:
    18→    """Get the N highest-priority open findings.
    19→
    20→    Legacy plan API intentionally returns only finding items (not synthetic
    21→    subjective queue items) so existing planner consumers stay stable.
    22→    The *tier* parameter is accepted for backward compatibility but ignored.
    23→    """
    24→    result = build_work_queue(
    25→        state,
    26→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    27→            count=count,
    28→            scan_path=scan_path,
    29→            status="open",
    30→            include_subjective=False,
    31→        ),
    32→    )
    33→    return [item for item in result["items"] if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    34→
    35→
    36→def get_next_item(
    37→    state: PlanState,
    38→    tier: int | None = None,
    39→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    40→) -> PlanItem | None:
    41→    """Get the highest-priority open finding."""
    42→    items = get_next_items(state, tier=tier, count=1, scan_path=scan_path)
    43→    return items[0] if items else None
    44→

1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    QueueBuildOptions,
    10→    WorkQueueResult,
    11→    build_work_queue,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    28→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    29→    count: int | None = None
    30→    scan_path: str | None = None
    31→    include_subjective: bool = True
    32→
    33→
    34→def build_open_plan_queue(
    35→    state: dict[str, Any],
    36→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    39→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    40→    resolved_scan_path: str | None
    41→    if isinstance(policy.scan_path, str):
    42→        resolved_scan_path = policy.scan_path
    43→    else:
    44→        raw_state_path = state.get("scan_path")
    45→        resolved_scan_path = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, str) else None
    46→    return build_work_queue(
    47→        state,
    48→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    49→            count=policy.count,
    50→            scan_path=resolved_scan_path,
    51→            status="open",
    52→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    53→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    54→        ),
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]

    74→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:

   178→
   179→def _plan_item_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   180→    """Build per-file sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   181→
   182→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   183→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   184→    raw_target = (
   185→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   186→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   187→        else 95
   188→    )
   189→    try:
   190→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   191→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   192→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   193→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   194→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   195→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   196→
   197→    queue = build_work_queue(
   198→        queue_state,
   199→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   200→            count=None,
   201→            scan_path=scan_path,
   202→            status="open",
   203→            include_subjective=True,
   204→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   205→        ),
   206→    )
   207→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   208→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   209→    for item in open_items:
   210→        by_file[item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   211→
   212→    lines: list[str] = []
   213→    total_count = len(open_items)
   214→    if not open_items:
   215→        return lines
   216→
   217→    lines.extend([
   218→        "---",
   219→        f"## Open Items ({total_count})",
   220→        "",
   221→    ])
   222→
   223→    sorted_files = sorted(
   224→        by_file.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→    )
   226→    for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→        lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→        lines.append("")
   230→        for item in file_items:
   231→            if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                    lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                continue
   237→
   238→            conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→            lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→            lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→        lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   247→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   248→
   249→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   250→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   251→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   252→    """
   253→    findings = state["findings"]
   254→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   255→
   256→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   257→    if plan is None:
   258→        try:
   259→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   260→            plan = load_plan()
   261→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   262→            plan = {}
   263→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   264→        plan = {}
   265→    plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   266→    plan.setdefault("skipped", {})
   267→    plan.setdefault("clusters", {})
   268→
   269→    has_plan = bool(
   270→        plan
   271→        and (
   272→            plan.get("queue_order")
   273→            or plan.get("skipped")
   274→            or plan.get("clusters")
   275→        )
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   279→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   280→    lines.extend(_summary_lines(stats))
   281→
   282→    if has_plan:
   283→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   284→        queue = build_work_queue(
   285→            state,
   286→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   287→                count=None,
   288→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   289→                status="open",
   290→                include_subjective=True,
   291→            ),
   292→        )
   293→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   294→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   295→
   296→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   297→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   298→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   299→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   300→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   301→        if remaining:
   302→            lines.append("---")
   303→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   304→            lines.append("")
   305→
   306→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   307→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   308→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   309→    else:
   310→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   311→
   312→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   313→
   314→    return "\n".join(lines)
   315→

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    22→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    23→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    24→        plan_data = plan
    25→
    26→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    27→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    28→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    29→        if active_cluster:
    30→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    31→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    32→
    33→
    34→def _print_queue_header(
    35→    *,
    36→    items: list[dict],
    37→    include_skipped: bool,
    38→    plan: dict,
    39→    plan_data: dict | None,
    40→    new_count: int = 0,
    41→) -> None:
    42→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    43→        QueueBreakdown,
    44→        format_queue_headline,
    45→    )
    46→
    47→    total = len(items)
    48→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    49→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    50→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    51→
    52→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    58→    plan_ordered = 0
    59→    if plan_data:
    60→        queue_order = plan_data.get("queue_order", [])
    61→        skipped_ids = set(plan_data.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    62→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    63→
    64→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    65→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    66→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    67→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    68→        subjective=subjective,
    69→    )
    70→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    71→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    72→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    73→
    74→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    75→    if focus:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    77→
    78→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    79→        return
    80→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    81→        return
    82→    print(
    83→        colorize(
    84→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    85→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    92→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    93→        return items[:top]
    94→    return items
    95→
    96→
    97→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    98→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    99→    _new = new_ids or set()
   100→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   101→        pos = str(idx)
   102→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   103→
   104→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   105→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   106→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   107→            conf_str = "—"
   108→            detector = "planning"
   109→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   110→            cluster_name = ""
   111→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   112→            conf_str = "—"
   113→            detector = "workflow"
   114→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   115→            cluster_name = ""
   116→        elif kind == "cluster":
   117→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   118→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   119→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   120→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   121→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   122→                if m.get("id") in _new
   123→            )
   124→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   125→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   126→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   127→        else:
   128→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   129→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   130→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   131→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   132→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   133→
   134→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   135→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   136→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   137→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   138→    return rows
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   143→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   144→    state = runtime.state
   145→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   149→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   150→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   151→
   152→    plan = load_plan()
   153→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   154→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   155→
   156→    queue = build_work_queue(
   157→        state,
   158→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   159→            count=None,
   160→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   161→            status="open",
   162→            include_subjective=True,
   163→            plan=plan_data,
   164→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   165→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   166→            collapse_clusters=True,
   167→        ),
   168→    )
   169→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   170→
   171→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   172→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   173→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last triage)
   174→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   175→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   176→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   177→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   178→
   179→    if sort_by == "recent":
   180→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   181→
   182→    _print_queue_header(
   183→        items=items,
   184→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   185→        plan=plan,
   186→        plan_data=plan_data,
   187→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   188→    )
   189→
   190→    if not items:
   191→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Determine which items to show
   195→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   196→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   197→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   198→
   199→    print()
   200→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   201→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   202→
   203→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   204→        remaining = len(items) - top
   205→        print(colorize(
   206→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   207→        ))
   208→    print()
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   212→

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import is_subjective_queue_item
    10→
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class QueueBreakdown:
    18→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    19→
    20→    queue_total: int = 0
    21→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    22→    skipped: int = 0
    23→    subjective: int = 0
    24→    workflow: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→
    30→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    plan: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    34→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    35→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    36→
    37→    result = build_work_queue(
    38→        state,
    39→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    40→            status="open",
    41→            count=None,
    42→            plan=plan,
    43→            collapse_clusters=True,
    44→            include_skipped=False,
    45→        ),
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    queue_total = result["total"]
    49→
    50→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
    51→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
    52→    items = result.get("items", [])
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for item in items
    55→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    56→    )
    57→    workflow = sum(
    58→        1 for item in items
    59→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    # Plan-derived counts
    63→    plan_ordered = 0
    64→    skipped = 0
    65→    if plan:
    66→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    67→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    68→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    71→
    72→    # Focus cluster info
    73→    focus_cluster = None
    74→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    75→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    76→    if plan:
    77→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→        if active:
    79→            focus_cluster = active
    80→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    81→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    83→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    84→            open_findings = {
    85→                fid
    86→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    87→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    88→            }
    89→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    90→
    91→    return QueueBreakdown(
    92→        queue_total=queue_total,
    93→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    94→        skipped=skipped,
    95→        subjective=subjective,
    96→        workflow=workflow,
    97→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    98→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
    99→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→
   107→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   108→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   109→
   110→    Examples::
   111→
   112→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   113→        Queue: 1934 items
   114→    """
   115→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   116→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   117→
   118→    # Parenthesized segments
   119→    segments: list[str] = []
   120→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   121→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   122→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   123→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   124→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   125→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   126→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   127→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   128→    if segments:
   129→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   130→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   131→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   132→    return label
   133→
   134→
   135→def format_queue_block(
   136→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   137→    *,
   138→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   139→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   140→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   141→
   142→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   143→    """
   144→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   145→
   146→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   147→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   148→        focus_line = (
   149→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   150→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   151→            f" items remaining"
   152→        )
   153→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   154→
   155→    # Frozen score line
   156→    if frozen_score is not None:
   157→        lines.append((
   158→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   159→            "cyan",
   160→        ))
   161→
   162→    # Queue headline — always the same
   163→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   164→
   165→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   166→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   167→        lines.append((
   168→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   169→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   170→            "dim",
   171→        ))
   172→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   173→        lines.append((
   174→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   175→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        ))
   178→    else:
   179→        lines.append((
   180→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   181→            "dim",
   182→        ))
   183→
   184→    return lines
   185→
   186→
   187→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   188→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   189→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   190→
   191→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   192→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
   193→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
   194→
   195→    result = build_work_queue(
   196→        state,
   197→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   198→            status="open",
   199→            count=None,
   200→            plan=plan,
   201→            collapse_clusters=True,
   202→            include_skipped=False,
   203→        ),
   204→    )
   205→    return result["total"]
   206→
   207→
   208→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   209→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   210→    if not plan:
   211→        return None
   212→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   213→
   214→
   215→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   216→    plan: dict,
   217→    queue_remaining: int,
   218→    *,
   219→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   222→
   223→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   224→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   225→    """
   226→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   227→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   228→    if strict is None:
   229→        return
   230→
   231→    if breakdown is not None:
   232→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   233→        print()
   234→        for text, style in block:
   235→            print(colorize(text, style))
   236→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   237→            print(colorize(
   238→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   239→                "dim",
   240→            ))
   241→        return
   242→
   243→    # Legacy fallback
   244→    print(
   245→        colorize(
   246→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   247→            "cyan",
   248→        )
   249→    )
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   253→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   254→            "dim",
   255→        )
   256→    )
   257→
   258→
   259→def _print_objective_drained_banner(
   260→    frozen_strict: float,
   261→    remaining: int,
   262→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   263→) -> None:
   264→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   265→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   266→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   267→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   268→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   269→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   270→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   271→    print(colorize(
   272→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   273→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   274→        "cyan",
   275→    ))
   276→    print(colorize(
   277→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   278→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   279→        "dim",
   280→    ))
   281→
   282→
   283→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   284→    state: dict,
   285→    prev,
   286→    plan: dict | None,
   287→) -> None:
   288→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   289→
   290→    Three display modes:
   291→
   292→    1. **Frozen** — objective queue items remain → show frozen plan-start score.
   293→    2. **Phase transition** — objective drained, subjective/workflow remains
   294→       → show live scores + transition banner.
   295→    3. **Live** — no active plan cycle or queue is clear → show live scores.
   296→    """
   297→    frozen_strict: float | None = None
   298→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   299→
   300→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   301→        frozen_strict = plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]
   302→        try:
   303→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   304→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   305→            pass
   306→
   307→    remaining = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   308→
   309→    # Frozen: objective work remains — show frozen plan-start score and return
   310→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   311→        objective_remaining = remaining - breakdown.subjective - breakdown.workflow
   312→        if objective_remaining > 0:
   313→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   314→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   315→            )
   316→            return
   317→
   318→    # Live scores (or phase transition): show current scores
   319→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   320→
   321→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   322→
   323→    # Phase transition: objective drained but subjective/workflow remains
   324→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   325→        _print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, remaining, breakdown)
   326→
   327→
   328→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   329→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   330→
   331→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   332→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   333→    """
   334→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   335→
   336→    try:
   337→        plan = load_plan()
   338→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   339→        plan = None
   340→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   341→
   342→
   343→__all__ = [
   344→    "QueueBreakdown",
   345→    "format_queue_block",
   346→    "format_queue_headline",
   347→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   348→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",

   350→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   351→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",

   353→]

1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     8→
     9→
    10→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    status: str,
    14→) -> bool:
    15→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    16→    if status != "fixed":
    17→        return False
    18→    try:
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return False
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    23→        if not queue_order:
    24→            return False
    25→
    26→        result = build_work_queue(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
    29→                plan=plan,
    30→                count=1,
    31→                collapse_clusters=True,
    32→                include_subjective=True,
    33→            ),
    34→        )
    35→        if not result["items"]:
    36→            return False
    37→
    38→        front_item = result["items"][0]
    39→        front_id = front_item["id"]
    40→
    41→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
    42→        if front_item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    43→            for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    44→                front_ids.add(member["id"])
    45→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    46→        else:
    47→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    48→
    49→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    50→        resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    51→        for pattern in patterns:
    52→            if pattern in clusters:
    53→                resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", []))
    54→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    55→            else:
    56→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    57→
    58→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    59→        resolved_ids = {
    60→            finding_id
    61→            for finding_id in resolved_ids
    62→            if finding_id in clusters
    63→            or (finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open")
    64→        }
    65→        if not resolved_ids:
    66→            return False
    67→
    68→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
    69→        for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    70→            if cluster_id in clusters:
    71→                alive_members = {
    72→                    finding_id
    73→                    for finding_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("finding_ids", [])
    74→                    if finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open"
    75→                }
    76→                if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    77→                    out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    78→        if not out_of_order:
    79→            return False
    80→
    81→        print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    82→        for finding_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    83→            print(f"    {finding_id}")
    84→        print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    85→        print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    86→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan move <pattern> --position top    # move to front", "dim"))
    87→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    89→        return True
    90→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    91→        return False
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
    95→

    78→
    79→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    80→    original_id: Required[str]
    81→    original_detector: str
    82→    original_file: str
    83→    original_summary: str
    84→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    85→    superseded_at: str
    86→    remapped_to: str | None
    87→    candidates: list[str]
    88→    note: str | None
    89→
    90→
    91→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    92→    version: Required[int]
    93→    created: Required[str]
    94→    updated: Required[str]
    95→    queue_order: list[str]
    96→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    97→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    98→    active_cluster: str | None
    99→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   100→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   101→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   102→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   103→    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle
   104→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   105→    epic_triage_meta: dict  # triage engine metadata
   106→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   107→    uncommitted_findings: list[str]
   108→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   109→
   110→
   111→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   112→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   113→    now = utc_now()
   114→    return {
   115→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   116→        "created": now,
   117→        "updated": now,
   118→        "queue_order": [],
   119→        "deferred": [],
   120→        "skipped": {},
   121→        "active_cluster": None,
   122→        "overrides": {},
   123→        "clusters": {},
   124→        "superseded": {},
   125→        "promoted_ids": [],
   126→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   127→        "execution_log": [],
   128→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   129→        "commit_log": [],
   130→        "uncommitted_findings": [],
   131→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   132→    }
   133→
   134→
   135→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   136→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   137→
   138→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   139→    v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters),
   140→    v5 (gates) to v6 (unified queue),
   141→    and v6 (synthesis naming) to v7 (triage naming).
   142→    """
   143→    defaults = empty_plan()
   144→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   145→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   146→
   147→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   148→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   149→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   150→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   151→    _migrate_v5_to_v6(plan)
   152→    _migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan)
   153→
   154→
   155→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   156→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   157→    return {
   158→        name: cluster
   159→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   160→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   161→    }
   162→
   163→
   164→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   165→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   166→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   167→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   168→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   169→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   170→
   171→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   172→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   173→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   174→    if overlap:
   175→        raise ValueError(
   176→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   177→        )
   178→
   179→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   180→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   181→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   182→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   183→            raise ValueError(
   184→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   185→            )
   186→
   187→
   188→__all__ = [
   189→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   190→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   191→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   192→    "Cluster",
   193→    "CommitRecord",
   194→    "ItemOverride",
   195→    "PlanModel",
   196→    "SkipEntry",
   197→    "SupersededEntry",
   198→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   199→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   200→    "empty_plan",
   201→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   202→    "triage_clusters",
   203→    "validate_plan",
   204→]
   205→
```

1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective findings and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective

    13→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError

    22→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    23→    state: StateModel,
    24→    *,
    25→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    26→) -> list[str]:
    27→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    28→
    29→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    30→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    31→
    32→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    33→    """
    34→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    35→    cleared: list[str] = []
    36→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    40→            continue
    41→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    42→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    43→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    44→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    45→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    46→    return cleared
    47→
    48→
    49→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    51→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    52→    scored: list[str] = []
    53→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    54→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    55→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    56→                scored.append(dim_key)
    57→        elif isinstance(assessment, (int, float)) and assessment:
    58→            scored.append(dim_key)
    59→    return sorted(scored)

    63→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""

    65→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")

    75→    normalized_dims = (
    76→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
    77→        if dimensions is not None
    78→        else None
    79→    )

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     6→
     7→
     8→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
     9→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    10→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    11→    if not scan_history:
    12→        return set()
    13→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    14→    if not threshold:
    15→        return set()
    16→    return {
    17→        finding_id
    18→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    19→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    20→    }
    21→
    22→
    23→def apply_plan_order(
    24→    items: list[dict],
    25→    plan: dict,
    26→    *,
    27→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    28→    cluster: str | None = None,
    29→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    30→) -> list[dict]:
    31→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    32→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    33→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    35→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    36→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    37→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→
    39→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    40→    for item in items:
    41→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    42→
    43→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    44→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    45→        if override.get("description"):
    46→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    47→        if override.get("note"):
    48→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    49→        if override.get("cluster"):
    50→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    51→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    52→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    53→                "name": cluster_name,
    54→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    55→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    56→            }
    57→
    58→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    59→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    60→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    61→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    62→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    63→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    64→
    65→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    66→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    67→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    68→    _new = new_ids or set()
    69→    for item in items:
    70→        item_id = item["id"]
    71→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    72→            continue
    73→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    74→            skipped_items.append(item)
    75→        elif item_id in _new:
    76→            remaining_new.append(item)
    77→        else:
    78→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    79→
    80→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    81→
    82→    # Triage stage IDs can appear in queue_order, so plan ordering may
    83→    # scramble their dependency order.  Re-sort just the stage items
    84→    # in-place to restore unblocked-before-blocked invariant.
    85→    stage_entries = [
    86→        (i, item) for i, item in enumerate(result)
    87→        if item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    88→    ]
    89→    if len(stage_entries) > 1:
    90→        sorted_stages = sorted(
    91→            [item for _, item in stage_entries],
    92→            key=lambda it: (
    93→                1 if it.get("is_blocked") else 0,
    94→                int(it.get("stage_index", 0)),
    95→            ),
    96→        )
    97→        for (idx, _), replacement in zip(stage_entries, sorted_stages):
    98→            result[idx] = replacement
    99→
   100→    if include_skipped:
   101→        result = result + skipped_items
   102→
   103→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
   104→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   105→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   106→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   107→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   108→            if skip_entry:
   109→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   110→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   111→                if skip_reason:
   112→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   113→
   114→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   115→    if effective_cluster:
   116→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   117→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   118→        if cluster_member_ids:
   119→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   120→    return result
   121→
   122→
   123→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   124→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   125→    try:
   126→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   127→
   128→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   129→        if meta:
   130→            return meta.action_type
   131→    except ImportError as exc:
   132→        _ = exc
   133→    return "manual_fix"
   134→
   135→
   136→def _build_cluster_meta(
   137→    cluster_name: str, members: list[dict], cluster_data: dict
   138→) -> dict:
   139→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   140→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   141→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   142→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   143→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   144→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   145→        action_type = "reorganize"
   146→    else:
   147→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   148→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   149→            action_type = "refactor"
   150→
   151→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   152→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   153→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   154→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   155→    else:
   156→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   157→
   158→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   159→    if not primary_command:
   160→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   161→
   162→    estimated_impact = max(
   163→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    return {
   167→        "id": cluster_name,
   168→        "kind": "cluster",
   169→        "action_type": action_type,
   170→        "summary": summary,
   171→        "members": members,
   172→        "member_count": len(members),
   173→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   174→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   175→        "cluster_auto": True,
   176→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   177→        "confidence": "high",
   178→        "detector": detector,
   179→        "file": "",
   180→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   181→    }
   182→
   183→
   184→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   186→
   187→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   188→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   189→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   190→    """
   191→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   192→    if not clusters:
   193→        return items
   194→
   195→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   196→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   197→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   198→            continue
   199→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   200→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   201→
   202→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   203→        return items
   204→
   205→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   206→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   207→    for item in items:
   208→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   209→        if cname:
   210→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   211→
   212→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   213→    meta_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
   214→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   215→        if len(members) < 2:
   216→            continue
   217→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   218→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   219→        )
   220→
   221→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   222→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   223→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   224→    result: list[dict] = []
   225→    for item in items:
   226→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   227→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   228→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   229→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   230→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   231→            # skip individual member
   232→        else:
   233→            result.append(item)
   234→    return result
   235→
   236→
   237→__all__ = [
   238→    "apply_plan_order",
   239→    "collapse_clusters",
   240→    "new_item_ids",
   241→]
   242→

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
     9→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    10→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    13→    ALL_STATUSES,
    14→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    15→    build_create_plan_item,
    16→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    17→    build_subjective_items,
    18→    build_triage_stage_items,
    19→    scope_matches,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    22→    build_finding_items,
    23→    enrich_with_impact,
    24→    group_queue_items,
    25→    item_explain,
    26→    item_sort_key,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    29→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    30→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    31→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    34→
    35→
    36→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    37→class QueueBuildOptions:
    38→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    39→
    40→    count: int | None = 1
    41→    scan_path: str | None = None
    42→    scope: str | None = None
    43→    status: str = "open"
    44→    include_subjective: bool = True
    45→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    46→    chronic: bool = False
    47→    explain: bool = False
    48→    plan: dict | None = None
    49→    include_skipped: bool = False
    50→    cluster: str | None = None
    51→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    52→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
    53→
    54→
    55→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    56→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    57→
    58→    items: list[dict]
    59→    total: int
    60→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    61→    new_ids: set[str]
    62→
    63→
    64→def build_work_queue(
    65→    state: StateModel,
    66→    *,
    67→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    68→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    69→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    70→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    71→
    72→    status = resolved_options.status
    73→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    74→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    75→    try:
    76→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    77→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    78→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    79→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    80→
    81→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    82→        state,
    83→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    84→        status_filter=status,
    85→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    86→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    87→    )
    88→
    89→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    90→
    91→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating
    92→    policy = resolved_options.policy or compute_subjective_visibility(state)
    93→
    94→    if (
    95→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    96→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    97→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    98→    ):
    99→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   100→            state,
   101→            state.get("findings", {}),
   102→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   103→        )
   104→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   105→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   106→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   107→            set(resolved_options.plan.get("queue_order", []))
   108→            if resolved_options.plan
   109→            else set()
   110→        )
   111→        for item in subjective_items:
   112→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   113→                continue
   114→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   115→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   116→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   117→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   118→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   119→                continue
   120→            all_items.append(item)
   121→
   122→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   123→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   124→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   125→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   126→        checkpoint_item = build_score_checkpoint_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   127→        if checkpoint_item is not None:
   128→            all_items.append(checkpoint_item)
   129→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   130→        if plan_item is not None:
   131→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   132→
   133→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   134→
   135→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   136→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   137→    all_items = [
   138→        item for item in all_items
   139→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   140→        or item.get("is_review")
   141→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   142→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   143→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   144→    ]
   145→
   146→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   147→
   148→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   149→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   150→    if resolved_options.plan:
   151→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   152→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   153→            all_items,
   154→            resolved_options.plan,
   155→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   156→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   157→            new_ids=new_ids,
   158→        )
   159→
   160→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   161→    should_collapse = (
   162→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   163→        and resolved_options.plan
   164→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   165→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   166→    )
   167→    if should_collapse:
   168→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   169→
   170→    total = len(all_items)
   171→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   172→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   173→
   174→    if resolved_options.explain:
   175→        for item in all_items:
   176→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   177→
   178→    return {
   179→        "items": all_items,
   180→        "total": total,
   181→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   182→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   183→    }
   184→
   185→
   186→__all__ = [
   187→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   188→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   189→    "WorkQueueResult",
   190→    "build_work_queue",
   191→    "group_queue_items",
   192→]
   193→
