1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    22→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    23→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    24→        plan_data = plan
    25→
    26→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    27→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    28→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    29→        if active_cluster:
    30→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    31→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    32→
    33→
    34→def _print_queue_header(
    35→    *,
    36→    items: list[dict],
    37→    include_skipped: bool,
    38→    plan: dict,
    39→    plan_data: dict | None,
    40→    new_count: int = 0,
    41→) -> None:
    42→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    43→        QueueBreakdown,
    44→        format_queue_headline,
    45→    )
    46→
    47→    total = len(items)
    48→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    49→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    50→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    51→
    52→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    58→    plan_ordered = 0
    59→    if plan_data:
    60→        queue_order = plan_data.get("queue_order", [])
    61→        skipped_ids = set(plan_data.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    62→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    63→
    64→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    65→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    66→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    67→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    68→        subjective=subjective,
    69→    )
    70→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    71→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    72→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    73→
    74→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    75→    if focus:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    77→
    78→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    79→        return
    80→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    81→        return
    82→    print(
    83→        colorize(
    84→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    85→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    92→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    93→        return items[:top]
    94→    return items
    95→
    96→
    97→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    98→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    99→    _new = new_ids or set()
   100→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   101→        pos = str(idx)
   102→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   103→
   104→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   105→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   106→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   107→            conf_str = "—"
   108→            detector = "planning"
   109→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   110→            cluster_name = ""
   111→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   112→            conf_str = "—"
   113→            detector = "workflow"
   114→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   115→            cluster_name = ""
   116→        elif kind == "cluster":
   117→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   118→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   119→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   120→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   121→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   122→                if m.get("id") in _new
   123→            )
   124→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   125→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   126→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   127→        else:
   128→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   129→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   130→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   131→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   132→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   133→
   134→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   135→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   136→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   137→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   138→    return rows
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   143→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   144→    state = runtime.state
   145→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   149→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   150→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   151→
   152→    plan = load_plan()
   153→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   154→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   155→
   156→    queue = build_work_queue(
   157→        state,
   158→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   159→            count=None,
   160→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   161→            status="open",
   162→            include_subjective=True,
   163→            plan=plan_data,
   164→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   165→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   166→            collapse_clusters=True,
   167→        ),
   168→    )
   169→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   170→
   171→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   172→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   173→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last triage)
   174→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   175→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   176→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   177→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   178→
   179→    if sort_by == "recent":
   180→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   181→
   182→    _print_queue_header(
   183→        items=items,
   184→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   185→        plan=plan,
   186→        plan_data=plan_data,
   187→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   188→    )
   189→
   190→    if not items:
   191→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Determine which items to show
   195→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   196→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   197→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   198→
   199→    print()
   200→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   201→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   202→
   203→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   204→        remaining = len(items) - top
   205→        print(colorize(
   206→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   207→        ))
   208→    print()
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   212→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    19→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    20→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    21→
    22→
    23→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    25→
    26→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    27→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    28→    """
    29→    if not dimension_scores:
    30→        for item in items:
    31→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    35→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    36→
    37→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    38→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    39→
    40→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    41→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    42→    for entry in entries:
    43→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    44→        if not name:
    45→            continue
    46→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    47→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    48→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    49→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    50→
    51→    for item in items:
    52→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    53→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    54→
    55→
    56→def _compute_item_impact(
    57→    item: dict,
    58→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    59→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    60→) -> float:
    61→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    62→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    63→
    64→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    65→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    66→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    67→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    68→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    69→        if entry:
    70→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    71→        return 0.0
    72→
    73→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    74→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    75→    if detector:
    76→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    77→        if dimension:
    78→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    79→            if entry:
    80→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]

1→"""State-backed work queue for review findings.
     2→
     3→Review findings live in state["findings"]. This module provides:
     4→- Listing/sorting open review findings by impact
     5→- Storing investigation notes on findings
     6→- Expiring stale holistic findings during scan
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    13→
    14→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import detail_dict
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→__all__ = [
    20→    "impact_label",
    21→    "list_open_review_findings",
    22→    "update_investigation",
    23→    "expire_stale_holistic",
    24→]
    25→
    26→
    27→def impact_label(weight: float) -> str:
    28→    """Convert weight to a human-readable impact label."""
    29→    try:
    30→        numeric = float(weight)
    31→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    32→        return "+"
    33→    if numeric >= 8:
    34→        return "+++"
    35→    if numeric >= 5:
    36→        return "++"
    37→    return "+"
    38→
    39→
    40→def list_open_review_findings(state: dict) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return open review findings sorted by impact (highest first)."""
    42→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    43→    review = [
    44→        finding
    45→        for finding in findings.values()
    46→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
    47→    ]
    48→
    49→    def _sort_key(finding: dict) -> tuple[float, str]:
    50→        weight, _impact, finding_id = finding_weight(finding)
    51→        return (-weight, finding_id)
    52→
    53→    review.sort(key=_sort_key)
    54→    return review
    55→
    56→
    57→def update_investigation(state: dict, finding_id: str, text: str) -> bool:
    58→    """Store investigation text on a finding. Returns False if not found/not open."""
    59→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    60→    if not finding or finding.get("status") != "open":
    61→        return False
    62→    detail = detail_dict(finding)
    63→    if not detail:
    64→        detail = {}
    65→        finding["detail"] = detail
    66→    detail["investigation"] = text
    67→    detail["investigated_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
    68→    return True
    69→
    70→
    71→def expire_stale_holistic(state: dict, max_age_days: int = 30) -> list[str]:
    72→    """Auto-resolve holistic review findings older than max_age_days."""
    73→    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    74→    expired: list[str] = []
    75→
    76→    for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    77→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    78→            continue
    79→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    80→            continue

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
     9→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    10→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    13→    ALL_STATUSES,
    14→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    15→    build_create_plan_item,
    16→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    17→    build_subjective_items,
    18→    build_triage_stage_items,
    19→    scope_matches,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    22→    build_finding_items,
    23→    enrich_with_impact,
    24→    group_queue_items,
    25→    item_explain,
    26→    item_sort_key,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    29→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    30→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    31→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    34→
    35→
    36→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    37→class QueueBuildOptions:
    38→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    39→
    40→    count: int | None = 1
    41→    scan_path: str | None = None
    42→    scope: str | None = None
    43→    status: str = "open"
    44→    include_subjective: bool = True
    45→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    46→    chronic: bool = False
    47→    explain: bool = False
    48→    plan: dict | None = None
    49→    include_skipped: bool = False
    50→    cluster: str | None = None
    51→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    52→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
    53→
    54→
    55→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    56→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    57→
    58→    items: list[dict]
    59→    total: int
    60→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    61→    new_ids: set[str]
    62→
    63→
    64→def build_work_queue(
    65→    state: StateModel,
    66→    *,
    67→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    68→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    69→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    70→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    71→
    72→    status = resolved_options.status
    73→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    74→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    75→    try:
    76→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    77→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    78→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    79→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    80→
    81→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    82→        state,
    83→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    84→        status_filter=status,
    85→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    86→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    87→    )
    88→
    89→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    90→
    91→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating
    92→    policy = resolved_options.policy or compute_subjective_visibility(state)
    93→
    94→    if (
    95→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    96→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    97→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    98→    ):
    99→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   100→            state,
   101→            state.get("findings", {}),
   102→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   103→        )
   104→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   105→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   106→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   107→            set(resolved_options.plan.get("queue_order", []))
   108→            if resolved_options.plan
   109→            else set()
   110→        )
   111→        for item in subjective_items:
   112→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   113→                continue
   114→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   115→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   116→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   117→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   118→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   119→                continue
   120→            all_items.append(item)
   121→
   122→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   123→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   124→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   125→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   126→        checkpoint_item = build_score_checkpoint_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   127→        if checkpoint_item is not None:
   128→            all_items.append(checkpoint_item)
   129→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   130→        if plan_item is not None:
   131→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   132→
   133→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   134→
   135→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   136→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   137→    all_items = [
   138→        item for item in all_items
   139→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   140→        or item.get("is_review")
   141→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   142→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   143→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   144→    ]
   145→
   146→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   147→
   148→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   149→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   150→    if resolved_options.plan:
   151→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   152→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   153→            all_items,
   154→            resolved_options.plan,
   155→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   156→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   157→            new_ids=new_ids,
   158→        )
   159→
   160→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   161→    should_collapse = (
   162→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   163→        and resolved_options.plan
   164→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   165→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   166→    )
   167→    if should_collapse:
   168→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   169→
   170→    total = len(all_items)
   171→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   172→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   173→
   174→    if resolved_options.explain:
   175→        for item in all_items:
   176→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   177→
   178→    return {
   179→        "items": all_items,
   180→        "total": total,
   181→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   182→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   183→    }
   184→
   185→
   186→__all__ = [
   187→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   188→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   189→    "WorkQueueResult",
   190→    "build_work_queue",
   191→    "group_queue_items",
   192→]
   193→

    31→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    33→    # Auto-resolve the create-plan workflow item when plan runs
    34→    plan = load_plan()
    35→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
    37→        save_plan(plan)
    38→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    45→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    46→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    47→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    48→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    49→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    50→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    52→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    53→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    54→
    55→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    56→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    57→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    58→
    59→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    60→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    61→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    62→    if total_skipped:
    63→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    64→    else:
    65→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    66→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    67→    if clusters:
    68→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    69→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    70→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    71→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    72→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    74→    if described or noted:
    75→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    76→    if active:
    77→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    78→    if superseded:
    79→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    80→
    81→    # Commit tracking summary
    82→    from desloppify.core.config import load_config as _load_config
    83→
    84→    _cfg = _load_config()
    85→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
    86→        from desloppify.engine.plan import commit_tracking_summary as _ct_summary
    87→
    88→        ct = _ct_summary(plan)
    89→        if ct["total"] > 0:
    90→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
    91→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
    92→            print(
    93→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
    94→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} findings){pr_str}"
    95→            )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    99→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   100→    plan = load_plan()
   101→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   102→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   103→    reset_plan(plan)
   104→    append_log_entry(
   105→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   106→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   107→    )
   108→    save_plan(plan)
   109→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   110→
   111→
   112→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   113→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   114→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   115→
   116→    if plan_action is None:
   117→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    if plan_action == "show":
   121→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if plan_action == "queue":
   125→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    if plan_action == "reset":
   129→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   130→        return
   131→
   132→    if plan_action == "move":
   133→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if plan_action in (
   137→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   138→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   139→    ):
   140→        dispatch = {
   141→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   142→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   143→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   144→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   145→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   146→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   147→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   148→        }
   149→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   153→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   154→        return
   155→
   156→    if plan_action == "triage":
   157→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_handlers import cmd_plan_triage
   158→
   159→        cmd_plan_triage(args)
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    if plan_action == "commit-log":
   163→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.commit_log_handlers import cmd_commit_log_dispatch
   164→
   165→        cmd_commit_log_dispatch(args)
   166→        return
   167→
   168→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   169→
   170→
   171→# Backwards-compatible alias
   172→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   173→
   174→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   175→
```

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     8→    PlanModel,
     9→    SkipEntry,
    10→    empty_plan,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    17→
    18→
    19→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    20→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    21→    try:
    22→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    23→
    24→        config = load_config()
    25→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    26→        return max(0, int(value))
    27→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    29→
    30→
    31→def append_log_entry(
    32→    plan: PlanModel,
    33→    action: str,
    34→    *,
    35→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    36→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    37→    actor: str = "user",
    38→    note: str | None = None,
    39→    detail: dict | None = None,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    42→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    43→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    44→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    45→        "action": action,
    46→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    47→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    48→        "actor": actor,
    49→        "note": note,
    50→        "detail": detail or {},
    51→    }
    52→    log.append(entry)
    53→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    54→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    55→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    56→
    57→
    58→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→# Helpers
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→
    62→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    63→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    64→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    65→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    66→    if finding_id in order:
    67→        order.remove(finding_id)
    68→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    69→
    70→
    71→def _resolve_position(
    72→    order: list[str],
    73→    position: str,
    74→    target: str | None = None,
    75→    offset: int | None = None,
    76→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    77→) -> int:
    78→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    79→
    80→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    81→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    82→    """
    83→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    84→
    85→    if position == "top":
    86→        return 0
    87→    if position == "bottom":
    88→        return len(order)
    89→
    90→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    91→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    92→
    93→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    94→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    95→            order,
    96→            moving,
    97→            position=position,
    98→            offset=offset,
    99→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
   100→        )
   101→
   102→    return len(order)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _resolve_relative_position(
   106→    order: list[str],
   107→    moving: set[str],
   108→    *,
   109→    position: str,
   110→    target: str,
   111→) -> int:
   112→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   113→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   114→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   115→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   119→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   120→        if item_id == target:
   121→            return i
   122→    return None
   123→
   124→
   125→def _resolve_offset_position(
   126→    order: list[str],
   127→    moving: set[str],
   128→    *,
   129→    position: str,
   130→    offset: int,
   131→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   132→) -> int:
   133→    if not finding_ids:
   134→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   135→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   136→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   137→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   138→    if current_idx is None:
   139→        if position == "up":
   140→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   141→        return len(clean_order)
   142→    if position == "up":
   143→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   144→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   145→
   146→
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→# Move items
   149→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→
   151→def move_items(
   152→    plan: PlanModel,
   153→    finding_ids: list[str],
   154→    position: str,
   155→    target: str | None = None,
   156→    offset: int | None = None,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→
   161→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   162→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   163→    finding_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   164→    if not finding_ids:
   165→        return 0
   166→
   167→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   168→
   169→    # Remove from skipped if present
   170→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   171→    for fid in finding_ids:
   172→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   173→
   174→    # Remove from current position in order
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        if fid in order:
   177→            order.remove(fid)
   178→
   179→    # Resolve insertion point
   180→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   181→
   182→    # Insert in original order
   183→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   184→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   185→
   186→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   187→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   188→    if position == "top":
   189→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   190→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   191→        for fid in finding_ids:
   192→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   193→                promoted.append(fid)
   194→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   195→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   196→
   197→    return len(finding_ids)
   198→
   199→
   200→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   201→# Skip / unskip
   202→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   203→
   204→def skip_items(
   205→    plan: PlanModel,
   206→    finding_ids: list[str],
   207→    *,
   208→    kind: str = "temporary",
   209→    reason: str | None = None,
   210→    note: str | None = None,
   211→    attestation: str | None = None,
   212→    review_after: int | None = None,
   213→    scan_count: int = 0,
   214→) -> int:
   215→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   216→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   217→    now = utc_now()
   218→    count = 0
   219→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   220→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   221→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   222→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   223→    for fid in finding_ids:
   224→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   225→        skipped[fid] = {
   226→            "finding_id": fid,
   227→            "kind": kind,
   228→            "reason": reason,
   229→            "note": note,
   230→            "attestation": attestation,
   231→            "created_at": now,
   232→            "review_after": review_after,
   233→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   234→        }
   235→        count += 1
   236→    return count
   237→
   238→
   239→def unskip_items(
   240→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   241→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   242→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   243→
   244→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   245→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   246→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   247→    """
   248→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   249→    count = 0
   250→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   251→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   252→    for fid in finding_ids:
   253→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   254→        if entry is not None:
   255→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   256→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   257→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   258→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   259→            count += 1
   260→    return count, need_reopen
   261→
   262→
   263→def resurface_stale_skips(
   264→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   265→) -> list[str]:
   266→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   267→
   268→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   269→    """
   270→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   271→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   272→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   273→    for fid in list(skipped):
   274→        entry = skipped[fid]
   275→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   276→            continue
   277→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   278→        if review_after is None:
   279→            continue
   280→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   281→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   282→            skipped.pop(fid)
   283→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   284→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   285→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   286→    return resurfaced
   287→
   288→
   289→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   290→# Describe / annotate
   291→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   292→
   293→def describe_finding(
   294→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   295→) -> None:
   296→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   297→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   298→    now = utc_now()
   299→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   300→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   301→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   302→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   303→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   304→
   305→
   306→def annotate_finding(
   307→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   308→) -> None:
   309→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   310→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   311→    now = utc_now()
   312→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   313→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   314→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   315→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   316→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   317→
   318→
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→# Clusters
   321→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   322→
   323→def create_cluster(
   324→    plan: PlanModel,
   325→    name: str,
   326→    description: str | None = None,
   327→    action: str | None = None,
   328→) -> Cluster:
   329→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   330→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   331→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   332→        raise ValueError(
   333→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   334→        )
   335→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   336→        raise ValueError(
   337→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for triage epics: {name!r}"
   338→        )
   339→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   340→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   341→    now = utc_now()
   342→    cluster: Cluster = {
   343→        "name": name,
   344→        "description": description,
   345→        "finding_ids": [],
   346→        "created_at": now,
   347→        "updated_at": now,
   348→        "auto": False,
   349→        "cluster_key": "",
   350→        "action": action,
   351→        "user_modified": False,
   352→    }
   353→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   354→    return cluster
   355→
   356→
   357→def add_to_cluster(
   358→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   359→) -> int:
   360→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   361→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   362→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   363→    if cluster is None:
   364→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   365→
   366→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   367→    count = 0
   368→    now = utc_now()
   369→    for fid in finding_ids:
   370→        if fid not in member_ids:
   371→            member_ids.append(fid)
   372→            count += 1
   373→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   374→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   375→        if fid not in overrides:
   376→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   377→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   378→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   379→
   380→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   381→    return count
   382→
   383→
   384→def remove_from_cluster(
   385→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   386→) -> int:
   387→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   390→    if cluster is None:
   391→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   392→
   393→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   394→    now = utc_now()
   395→    count = 0
   396→    for fid in finding_ids:
   397→        if fid in member_ids:
   398→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   399→            count += 1
   400→        # Clear cluster from override
   401→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   402→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   403→            override["cluster"] = None
   404→            override["updated_at"] = now
   405→
   406→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   407→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   408→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   409→
   410→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   411→    return count
   412→
   413→
   414→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   415→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   416→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   417→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   418→    if cluster is None:
   419→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   420→
   421→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   422→    now = utc_now()
   423→    for fid in orphaned:
   424→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   425→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   426→            override["cluster"] = None
   427→            override["updated_at"] = now
   428→
   429→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   430→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   431→
   432→    return orphaned
   433→
   434→
   435→def merge_clusters(
   436→    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str
   437→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   438→    """Move all source findings to target, copy missing metadata, delete source.
   439→
   440→    Returns ``(added_count, source_finding_ids)``.
   441→    """
   442→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   443→    if source_name == target_name:
   444→        raise ValueError("Cannot merge a cluster into itself")
   445→    source = plan["clusters"].get(source_name)
   446→    if source is None:
   447→        raise ValueError(f"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist")
   448→    target = plan["clusters"].get(target_name)
   449→    if target is None:
   450→        raise ValueError(f"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist")
   451→
   452→    source_ids = list(source.get("finding_ids", []))
   453→    target_ids: list[str] = target["finding_ids"]
   454→    now = utc_now()
   455→
   456→    # Add source findings to target (deduplicate)
   457→    existing = set(target_ids)
   458→    added = 0
   459→    for fid in source_ids:
   460→        if fid not in existing:
   461→            target_ids.append(fid)
   462→            existing.add(fid)
   463→            added += 1
   464→        # Update override to point to target cluster
   465→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   466→        if fid not in overrides:
   467→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   468→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = target_name
   469→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   470→
   471→    # Copy metadata from source if target is missing them
   472→    if not target.get("description") and source.get("description"):
   473→        target["description"] = source["description"]
   474→    if not target.get("action_steps") and source.get("action_steps"):
   475→        target["action_steps"] = list(source["action_steps"])
   476→    if not target.get("action") and source.get("action"):
   477→        target["action"] = source["action"]
   478→
   479→    target["updated_at"] = now
   480→
   481→    # Delete source cluster
   482→    plan["clusters"].pop(source_name, None)
   483→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == source_name:
   484→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   485→
   486→    return added, source_ids
   487→
   488→
   489→def move_cluster(
   490→    plan: PlanModel,
   491→    cluster_name: str,
   492→    position: str,
   493→    target: str | None = None,
   494→    offset: int | None = None,
   495→) -> int:
   496→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   497→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   498→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   499→    if cluster is None:
   500→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   501→
   502→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   503→    if not member_ids:
   504→        return 0
   505→
   506→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   507→
   508→
   509→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   510→# Focus
   511→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   512→
   513→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   514→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   515→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   516→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   517→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   518→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   519→
   520→
   521→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   522→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   523→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   524→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   525→
   526→
   527→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   528→# Reset
   529→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   530→
   531→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   532→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.
   533→
   534→    Sets ``plan_start_scores`` to a sentinel so the next scan seeds real
   535→    scores instead of incorrectly treating the reset as a completed cycle.
   536→    """
   537→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   538→    plan.clear()
   539→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   540→        plan[k] = v
   541→    plan["created"] = created
   542→    # Sentinel: truthy so ``not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`` is False,
   543→    # but recognized by _seed_plan_start_scores as needing replacement.
   544→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"reset": True}
   545→
   546→
   547→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   548→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   549→
   550→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   551→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   552→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   553→    """
   554→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   555→    found = 0
   556→
   557→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   558→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   559→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   560→
   561→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   562→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   563→    for fid in finding_ids:
   564→        was_present = False
   565→        if fid in order:
   566→            order.remove(fid)
   567→            was_present = True
   568→        if fid in skipped:
   569→            skipped.pop(fid)
   570→            was_present = True
   571→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   572→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   573→            if fid in ids:
   574→                ids.remove(fid)
   575→                was_present = True
   576→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   577→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   578→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   579→            override["cluster"] = None
   580→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   581→        if was_present:
   582→            found += 1
   583→
   584→    return found
   585→
   586→
   587→__all__ = [
   588→    "add_to_cluster",
   589→    "annotate_finding",
   590→    "append_log_entry",
   591→    "clear_focus",
   592→    "create_cluster",
   593→    "delete_cluster",
   594→    "describe_finding",
   595→    "merge_clusters",
   596→    "move_cluster",
   597→    "move_items",
   598→    "purge_ids",
   599→    "remove_from_cluster",
   600→    "reset_plan",
   601→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   602→    "set_focus",
   603→    "skip_items",
   604→    "unskip_items",
   605→]
   606→
```

    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    52→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    53→        return True
    54→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    55→        members = item.get("members", [])
    56→        return bool(members) and all(
    57→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    58→        )
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    63→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    64→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    65→    weight_by_confidence = {
    66→        "high": 1.0,
    67→        "medium": 0.7,
    68→        "low": 0.3,
    69→    }
    70→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    71→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    72→        weight *= 10.0
    73→    return float(weight)
    74→
    75→
    76→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    77→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    78→    if not scope:
    79→        return True
    80→
    81→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    82→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    83→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    84→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    85→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    86→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    87→
    88→    if "*" in scope:
    89→        return any(
    90→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    91→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    92→        )
    93→
    94→    if "::" in scope:
    95→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    96→
    97→    lowered = scope.lower()
    98→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        return (
   100→            lowered in item_id.lower()
   101→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
   102→            or lowered in summary.lower()
   103→        )
   104→
   105→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   106→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   107→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   108→
   109→    return (
   110→        detector == scope
   111→        or filepath == scope
   112→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   113→    )

   116→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   117→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   118→
   119→
   120→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   121→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   122→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   123→    target = cleaned.lower()
   124→
   125→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   126→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   127→            return str(dim_key)
   128→    return slugify(cleaned)
   129→

   132→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   133→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   134→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   135→    return {
   136→        cleaned.lower(),

   138→        slugify(cleaned),
   139→        canonical.lower(),

   144→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   145→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   146→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   147→    if not lang:

   150→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   151→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):

   153→
   154→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   155→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   156→        return None
   157→
   158→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   159→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):

   161→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}

   164→def primary_command_for_finding(
   165→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   166→) -> str:
   167→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   168→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   169→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   170→        available_fixers = [
   171→            fixer
   172→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   173→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   174→        ]
   175→        if available_fixers:
   176→            return f"desloppify autofix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   177→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   178→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   179→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   180→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   181→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'

   184→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   186→
   187→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   188→    """
   189→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   190→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   191→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   192→    )
   193→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   194→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   195→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   199→    order_set = set(order)
   200→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   201→    if not present:
   202→        return []
   203→
   204→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   205→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   206→
   207→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   208→    open_review_count = sum(
   209→        1 for f in findings.values()
   210→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   211→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   212→    )
   213→
   214→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   215→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   216→
   217→    items: list[dict] = []
   218→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   219→        if sid not in present:
   220→            continue
   221→        if name in confirmed:
   222→            continue
   223→
   224→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   225→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   226→        blocked_by = sorted(
   227→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   228→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   232→        if name == "commit":
   233→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   234→
   235→        items.append({
   236→            "id": sid,
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "confidence": "high",
   239→            "detector": "triage",
   240→            "file": ".",
   241→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   242→            "stage_name": name,
   243→            "stage_index": idx,
   244→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   247→                "stage": name,
   248→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   249→            },
   250→            "primary_command": cmd,
   251→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   252→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   253→        })
   254→    return items
   255→

   257→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   258→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if it's in the queue.
   259→
   260→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   261→    """
   262→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID
   263→
   264→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   265→        return None
   266→
   267→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   268→
   269→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   270→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   271→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   272→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   273→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   274→
   275→    return {
   276→        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   277→        "tier": 1,
   278→        "confidence": "high",
   279→        "detector": "workflow",
   280→        "file": ".",
   281→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   282→        "summary": f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   283→        "detail": {
   284→            "strict": strict,
   285→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   286→            "delta": delta,
   287→        },
   288→        "primary_command": f'desloppify plan done "{WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID}" --note "Reviewed score checkpoint" --confirm',
   289→        "blocked_by": [],
   290→        "is_blocked": False,
   291→    }

   294→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> dict | None:
   295→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if it's in the queue.
   296→
   297→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   298→    """
   299→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID
   300→
   301→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   302→        return None
   303→
   304→    return {
   305→        "id": WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   306→        "tier": 1,
   307→        "confidence": "high",
   308→        "detector": "workflow",
   309→        "file": ".",
   310→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   311→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",
   312→        "detail": {},
   313→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan",
   314→        "blocked_by": [],
   315→        "is_blocked": False,
   316→    }

   318→
   319→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   320→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   321→    if not dim_scores:
   322→        return {}
   323→
   324→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   325→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   326→    for entry in entries:
   327→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   328→        if not name:
   329→            continue
   330→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   331→        [REDACTED](name)
   332→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   333→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   334→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   335→            if not key:
   336→                continue
   337→            aliases.add(key)
   338→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   339→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   340→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   341→        for alias in aliases:
   342→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   343→    return scores
   344→
   345→
   346→def build_subjective_items(
   347→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   348→) -> list[dict]:
   349→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   350→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   351→    if not dim_scores:
   352→        return []
   353→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   354→
   355→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   356→    if not subjective_entries:
   357→        return []
   358→    unassessed_dims = {
   359→        str(name).strip()
   360→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   361→            dim_scores
   362→        )
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   366→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   367→    for finding in findings.values():
   368→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   369→            continue
   370→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   371→        if not dim_key:
   372→            continue
   373→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   374→
   375→    items: list[dict] = []
   376→    def _prepare_command(
   377→        cli_keys: list[str],
   378→        *,
   379→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   380→    ) -> str:
   381→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   382→        if cli_keys:
   383→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   384→        if force_review_rerun:
   385→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   386→        return command
   387→
   388→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   389→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   390→        if not name:
   391→            continue
   392→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   394→            continue
   395→
   396→        [REDACTED](name)
   397→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   398→        cli_keys = [
   399→            str(key).strip().lower()
   400→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   401→            if str(key).strip()
   402→        ]
   403→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   404→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   405→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   406→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   407→            name in unassessed_dims
   408→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   409→        )
   410→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   411→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   412→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   413→        if open_review > 0:
   414→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   415→        else:
   416→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   417→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   418→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   419→        items.append(
   420→            {
   421→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   422→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   423→                "file": ".",
   424→                "confidence": "medium",
   425→                "summary": summary,
   426→                "detail": {
   427→                    "dimension_name": name,
   428→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   429→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   430→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   431→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   432→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   433→                },
   434→                "status": "open",
   435→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   436→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   437→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   438→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   439→            }
   440→        )
   441→    return items
   442→
   443→
   444→__all__ = [
   445→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   446→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   447→    "build_create_plan_item",
   448→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   449→    "build_subjective_items",
   450→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   451→    "detail_dict",

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
