```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    all_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    22→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    23→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    52→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    53→        return True
    54→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    55→        members = item.get("members", [])
    56→        return bool(members) and all(
    57→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    58→        )
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    63→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    64→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    65→    weight_by_confidence = {
    66→        "high": 1.0,
    67→        "medium": 0.7,
    68→        "low": 0.3,
    69→    }
    70→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    71→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    72→        weight *= 10.0
    73→    return float(weight)
    74→
    75→
    76→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    77→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    78→    if not scope:
    79→        return True
    80→
    81→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    82→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    83→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    84→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    85→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    86→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    87→
    88→    if "*" in scope:
    89→        return any(
    90→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    91→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    92→        )
    93→
    94→    if "::" in scope:
    95→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    96→
    97→    lowered = scope.lower()
    98→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        return (
   100→            lowered in item_id.lower()
   101→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
   102→            or lowered in summary.lower()
   103→        )
   104→
   105→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   106→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   107→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   108→
   109→    return (
   110→        detector == scope
   111→        or filepath == scope
   112→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   113→    )
   114→
   115→
   116→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   117→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   118→
   119→
   120→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   121→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   122→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   123→    target = cleaned.lower()
   124→
   125→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   126→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   127→            return str(dim_key)
   128→    return slugify(cleaned)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   132→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   133→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   134→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   135→    return {
   136→        cleaned.lower(),
   137→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   138→        slugify(cleaned),
   139→        canonical.lower(),
   140→        slugify(canonical),
   141→    }
   142→
   143→
   144→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   145→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   146→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   147→    if not lang:
   148→        return None
   149→
   150→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   151→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   155→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   156→        return None
   157→
   158→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   159→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   160→        return None
   161→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   162→
   163→
   164→def primary_command_for_finding(
   165→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   166→) -> str:
   167→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   168→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   169→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   170→        available_fixers = [
   171→            fixer
   172→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   173→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   174→        ]
   175→        if available_fixers:
   176→            return f"desloppify autofix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   177→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   178→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   179→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   180→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   181→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   182→
   183→
   184→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   186→
   187→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   188→    """
   189→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   190→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   191→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   192→    )
   193→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   194→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   195→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   199→    order_set = set(order)
   200→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   201→    if not present:
   202→        return []
   203→
   204→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   205→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   206→
   207→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   208→    open_review_count = sum(
   209→        1 for f in findings.values()
   210→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   211→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   212→    )
   213→
   214→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   215→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   216→
   217→    items: list[dict] = []
   218→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   219→        if sid not in present:
   220→            continue
   221→        if name in confirmed:
   222→            continue
   223→
   224→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   225→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   226→        blocked_by = sorted(
   227→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   228→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   232→        if name == "commit":
   233→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   234→
   235→        items.append({
   236→            "id": sid,
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "confidence": "high",
   239→            "detector": "triage",
   240→            "file": ".",
   241→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   242→            "stage_name": name,
   243→            "stage_index": idx,
   244→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   247→                "stage": name,
   248→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   249→            },
   250→            "primary_command": cmd,
   251→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   252→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   253→        })
   254→    return items
   255→
   256→
   257→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   258→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if it's in the queue.
   259→
   260→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   261→    """
   262→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID
   263→
   264→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   265→        return None
   266→
   267→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   268→
   269→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   270→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   271→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   272→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   273→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   274→
   275→    return {
   276→        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   277→        "tier": 1,
   278→        "confidence": "high",
   279→        "detector": "workflow",
   280→        "file": ".",
   281→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   282→        "summary": f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   283→        "detail": {
   284→            "strict": strict,
   285→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   286→            "delta": delta,
   287→        },
   288→        "primary_command": f'desloppify plan done "{WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID}" --note "Reviewed score checkpoint" --confirm',
   289→        "blocked_by": [],
   290→        "is_blocked": False,
   291→    }
   292→
   293→
   294→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> dict | None:
   295→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if it's in the queue.
   296→
   297→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   298→    """
   299→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID
   300→
   301→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   302→        return None
   303→
   304→    return {
   305→        "id": WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   306→        "tier": 1,
   307→        "confidence": "high",
   308→        "detector": "workflow",
   309→        "file": ".",
   310→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   311→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",
   312→        "detail": {},
   313→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan",
   314→        "blocked_by": [],
   315→        "is_blocked": False,
   316→    }
   317→
   318→
   319→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   320→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   321→    if not dim_scores:
   322→        return {}
   323→
   324→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   325→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   326→    for entry in entries:
   327→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   328→        if not name:
   329→            continue
   330→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   331→        [REDACTED](name)
   332→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   333→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   334→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   335→            if not key:
   336→                continue
   337→            aliases.add(key)
   338→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   339→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   340→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   341→        for alias in aliases:
   342→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   343→    return scores
   344→
   345→
   346→def build_subjective_items(
   347→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   348→) -> list[dict]:
   349→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   350→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   351→    if not dim_scores:
   352→        return []
   353→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   354→
   355→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   356→    if not subjective_entries:
   357→        return []
   358→    unassessed_dims = {
   359→        str(name).strip()
   360→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   361→            dim_scores
   362→        )
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   366→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   367→    for finding in findings.values():
   368→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   369→            continue
   370→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   371→        if not dim_key:
   372→            continue
   373→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   374→
   375→    items: list[dict] = []
   376→    def _prepare_command(
   377→        cli_keys: list[str],
   378→        *,
   379→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   380→    ) -> str:
   381→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   382→        if cli_keys:
   383→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   384→        if force_review_rerun:
   385→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   386→        return command
   387→
   388→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   389→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   390→        if not name:
   391→            continue
   392→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   394→            continue
   395→
   396→        [REDACTED](name)
   397→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   398→        cli_keys = [
   399→            str(key).strip().lower()
   400→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   401→            if str(key).strip()
   402→        ]
   403→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   404→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   405→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   406→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   407→            name in unassessed_dims
   408→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   409→        )
   410→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   411→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   412→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   413→        if open_review > 0:
   414→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   415→        else:
   416→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   417→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   418→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   419→        items.append(
   420→            {
   421→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   422→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   423→                "file": ".",
   424→                "confidence": "medium",
   425→                "summary": summary,
   426→                "detail": {
   427→                    "dimension_name": name,
   428→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   429→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   430→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   431→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   432→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   433→                },
   434→                "status": "open",
   435→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   436→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   437→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   438→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   439→            }
   440→        )
   441→    return items
   442→
   443→
   444→__all__ = [
   445→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   446→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   447→    "build_create_plan_item",
   448→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   449→    "build_subjective_items",
   450→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   451→    "detail_dict",
   452→    "is_review_finding",
```

```
     2→

    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS

    22→
    23→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    25→
    26→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    27→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    28→    """
    29→    if not dimension_scores:
    30→        for item in items:
    31→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    35→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    36→
    37→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    38→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    39→
    40→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    41→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    42→    for entry in entries:
    43→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    44→        if not name:
    45→            continue
    46→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    47→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    48→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    49→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    50→
    51→    for item in items:
    52→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    53→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    54→
    55→
    56→def _compute_item_impact(
    57→    item: dict,
    58→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    59→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    60→) -> float:
    61→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    62→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    63→
    64→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    65→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    66→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    67→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    68→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    69→        if entry:
    70→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    71→        return 0.0
    72→
    73→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    74→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    75→    if detector:
    76→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    77→        if dimension:
    78→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    79→            if entry:
    80→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    81→
    82→    return 0.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    86→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    88→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    89→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    90→
    91→
    92→def build_finding_items(
    93→    state: dict,
    94→    *,
    95→    scan_path: str | None,
    96→    status_filter: str,
    97→    scope: str | None,
    98→    chronic: bool,
    99→) -> list[dict]:
   100→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
   101→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   102→    out: list[dict] = []
   103→
   104→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   105→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   106→            continue
   107→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   108→            continue
   109→        if chronic and not (
   110→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   111→        ):
   112→            continue
   113→
   114→        # Evidence-only: skip findings below standalone confidence threshold
   115→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   116→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   117→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   118→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   119→            finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   120→            if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
   121→                continue
   122→
   123→        item = dict(finding)
   124→        item["id"] = finding_id
   125→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   126→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   127→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   128→        item["review_weight"] = (
   129→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   130→        )
   131→        subjective_score = None
   132→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   133→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   134→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   135→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   136→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   137→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   138→            )
   139→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   140→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   141→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   142→            item,
   143→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   144→        )
   145→
   146→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   147→            continue
   148→        out.append(item)
   149→
   150→    return out
   151→
   152→
   153→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   154→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   155→
   156→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
   157→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   158→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   159→
   160→    # Triage stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
   161→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   162→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   163→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   164→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   165→
   166→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
   167→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   168→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   169→
   170→    if kind == "cluster":
   171→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   172→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   173→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   174→        )
   175→        return (
   176→            0,
   177→            action_pri,
   178→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   179→            item.get("id", ""),
   180→        )
   181→
   182→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   183→
   184→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   185→        return (
   186→            1,
   187→            -impact,
   188→            subjective_score_value(item),
   189→            item.get("id", ""),
   190→        )
   191→
   192→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   193→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   194→    return (
   195→        1,
   196→        -impact,
   197→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   198→        -review_weight,
   199→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   200→        item.get("id", ""),
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   205→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   206→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   207→        return {
   208→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   209→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   210→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   211→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   212→            "policy": "Triage stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   213→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   214→        }
   215→
   216→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   217→        return {
   218→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   219→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after triage stages, before findings.",
   220→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   221→        }
   222→
   223→    if kind == "cluster":
   224→        return {
   225→            "kind": "cluster",
   226→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   227→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
   228→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", 0),
   229→            "policy": "Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type then size.",
   230→            "ranking_factors": ["action_type asc", "member_count desc", "id asc"],
   231→        }
   232→
   233→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   234→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   235→        return {
   236→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   237→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   238→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   239→            "initial_review": initial,
   240→            "policy": (
   241→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   242→                if initial else
   243→                "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   244→            ),
   245→            "ranking_factors": ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   246→        }
   247→
   248→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   249→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   250→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   251→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   252→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   253→    if is_subjective:
   254→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   255→    elif is_review:
   256→        ranking_factors = [
   257→            "estimated_impact desc",
   258→            "confidence asc",
   259→            "review_weight desc",
   260→            "count desc",
   261→            "id asc",
   262→        ]
   263→    else:
   264→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   265→    explain = {
   266→        "kind": "finding",
   267→        "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   268→        "confidence": confidence,
   269→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   270→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   271→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   272→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   273→    }
   274→    if is_review:
   275→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   276→    if is_subjective:
   277→        explain["policy"] = (
   278→            "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   279→        )
   280→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   281→    return explain
   282→
   283→
   284→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   285→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   286→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   287→    for item in items:
   288→        if group == "file":
   289→            key = item.get("file", "")
   290→        elif group == "detector":
   291→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   292→        elif group == "cluster":
   293→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   294→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   295→        else:
   296→            key = "items"
   297→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   298→    return grouped
   299→
   300→
   301→__all__ = [
   302→    "build_finding_items",
   303→    "enrich_with_impact",
   304→    "item_explain",
   305→    "item_sort_key",
   306→    "subjective_score_value",
   307→    "group_queue_items",
   308→]
   309→
```

```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     6→
     7→
     8→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
     9→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    10→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    11→    if not scan_history:
    12→        return set()
    13→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    14→    if not threshold:
    15→        return set()
    16→    return {
    17→        finding_id
    18→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    19→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    20→    }
    21→
    22→
    23→def apply_plan_order(
    24→    items: list[dict],
    25→    plan: dict,
    26→    *,
    27→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    28→    cluster: str | None = None,
    29→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    30→) -> list[dict]:
    31→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    32→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    33→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    35→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    36→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    37→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→
    39→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    40→    for item in items:
    41→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    42→
    43→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    44→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    45→        if override.get("description"):
    46→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    47→        if override.get("note"):
    48→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    49→        if override.get("cluster"):
    50→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    51→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    52→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    53→                "name": cluster_name,
    54→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    55→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    56→            }
    57→
    58→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    59→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    60→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    61→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    62→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    63→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    64→
    65→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    66→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    67→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    68→    _new = new_ids or set()
    69→    for item in items:
    70→        item_id = item["id"]
    71→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    72→            continue
    73→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    74→            skipped_items.append(item)
    75→        elif item_id in _new:
    76→            remaining_new.append(item)
    77→        else:
    78→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    79→
    80→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    81→
    82→    # Triage stage IDs can appear in queue_order, so plan ordering may
    83→    # scramble their dependency order.  Re-sort just the stage items
    84→    # in-place to restore unblocked-before-blocked invariant.
    85→    stage_entries = [
    86→        (i, item) for i, item in enumerate(result)
    87→        if item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    88→    ]
    89→    if len(stage_entries) > 1:
    90→        sorted_stages = sorted(
    91→            [item for _, item in stage_entries],
    92→            key=lambda it: (
    93→                1 if it.get("is_blocked") else 0,
    94→                int(it.get("stage_index", 0)),
    95→            ),
    96→        )
    97→        for (idx, _), replacement in zip(stage_entries, sorted_stages):
    98→            result[idx] = replacement
    99→
   100→    if include_skipped:
   101→        result = result + skipped_items
   102→
   103→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
   104→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   105→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   106→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   107→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   108→            if skip_entry:
   109→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   110→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   111→                if skip_reason:
   112→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   113→
   114→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   115→    if effective_cluster:
   116→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   117→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   118→        if cluster_member_ids:
   119→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   120→    return result
   121→
   122→
   123→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   124→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   125→    try:
   126→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   127→
   128→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   129→        if meta:
   130→            return meta.action_type
   131→    except ImportError as exc:
   132→        _ = exc
   133→    return "manual_fix"
   134→
   135→
   136→def _build_cluster_meta(
   137→    cluster_name: str, members: list[dict], cluster_data: dict
   138→) -> dict:
   139→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   140→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   141→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   142→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   143→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   144→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   145→        action_type = "reorganize"
   146→    else:
   147→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   148→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   149→            action_type = "refactor"
   150→
   151→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   152→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   153→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   154→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   155→    else:
   156→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   157→
   158→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   159→    if not primary_command:
   160→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   161→
   162→    estimated_impact = max(
   163→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    return {
   167→        "id": cluster_name,
   168→        "kind": "cluster",
   169→        "action_type": action_type,
   170→        "summary": summary,
   171→        "members": members,
   172→        "member_count": len(members),
   173→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   174→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   175→        "cluster_auto": True,
   176→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   177→        "confidence": "high",
   178→        "detector": detector,
   179→        "file": "",
   180→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   181→    }
   182→
   183→
   184→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   186→
   187→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   188→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   189→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   190→    """
   191→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   192→    if not clusters:
   193→        return items
   194→
   195→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   196→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   197→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   198→            continue
   199→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   200→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   201→
   202→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   203→        return items
   204→
   205→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   206→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   207→    for item in items:
   208→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   209→        if cname:
   210→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   211→
   212→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   213→    meta_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
   214→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   215→        if len(members) < 2:
   216→            continue
   217→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   218→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   219→        )
   220→
   221→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   222→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   223→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   224→    result: list[dict] = []
   225→    for item in items:
   226→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   227→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   228→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   229→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   230→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   231→            # skip individual member
   232→        else:
   233→            result.append(item)
   234→    return result
   235→
   236→
   237→__all__ = [
   238→    "apply_plan_order",
   239→    "collapse_clusters",
   240→    "new_item_ids",
   241→]
   242→
```

```
1→"""State-backed work queue for review findings.
     2→
     3→Review findings live in state["findings"]. This module provides:
     4→- Listing/sorting open review findings by impact
     5→- Storing investigation notes on findings
     6→- Expiring stale holistic findings during scan
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    13→
    14→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import detail_dict
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→__all__ = [
    20→    "impact_label",
    21→    "list_open_review_findings",
    22→    "update_investigation",
    23→    "expire_stale_holistic",
    24→]
    25→
    26→
    27→def impact_label(weight: float) -> str:
    28→    """Convert weight to a human-readable impact label."""
    29→    try:
    30→        numeric = float(weight)
    31→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    32→        return "+"
    33→    if numeric >= 8:
    34→        return "+++"
    35→    if numeric >= 5:
    36→        return "++"
    37→    return "+"
    38→
    39→
    40→def list_open_review_findings(state: dict) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return open review findings sorted by impact (highest first)."""
    42→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    43→    review = [
    44→        finding
    45→        for finding in findings.values()
    46→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
    47→    ]
    48→
    49→    def _sort_key(finding: dict) -> tuple[float, str]:
    50→        weight, _impact, finding_id = finding_weight(finding)
    51→        return (-weight, finding_id)
    52→
    53→    review.sort(key=_sort_key)
    54→    return review
    55→
    56→
    57→def update_investigation(state: dict, finding_id: str, text: str) -> bool:
    58→    """Store investigation text on a finding. Returns False if not found/not open."""
    59→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    60→    if not finding or finding.get("status") != "open":
    61→        return False
    62→    detail = detail_dict(finding)
    63→    if not detail:
    64→        detail = {}
    65→        finding["detail"] = detail
    66→    detail["investigation"] = text
    67→    detail["investigated_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
    68→    return True
    69→
    70→
    71→def expire_stale_holistic(state: dict, max_age_days: int = 30) -> list[str]:
    72→    """Auto-resolve holistic review findings older than max_age_days."""
    73→    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    74→    expired: list[str] = []
    75→
    76→    for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    77→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    78→            continue
    79→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    80→            continue
    81→        if not detail_dict(finding).get("holistic"):
    82→            continue
    83→
    84→        last_seen = finding.get("last_seen")
    85→        if not last_seen:
    86→            continue
    87→
    88→        try:
    89→            seen_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_seen)
    90→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    91→            logger.debug(
    92→                "Skipping holistic finding %s with invalid last_seen %r: %s",
    93→                finding_id,
    94→                last_seen,
    95→                exc,
    96→            )
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        age_days = (now - seen_dt).days
   100→        if age_days > max_age_days:
   101→            finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   102→            finding["resolved_at"] = now.isoformat()
   103→            finding["note"] = "holistic review expired — re-run review to re-evaluate"
   104→            expired.append(finding_id)
   105→
   106→    return expired
   107→
```

```
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    22→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    23→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    24→        plan_data = plan
    25→
    26→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    27→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    28→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    29→        if active_cluster:
    30→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    31→    return plan_data, effective_cluster

    34→def _print_queue_header(
    35→    *,
    36→    items: list[dict],
    37→    include_skipped: bool,
    38→    plan: dict,
    39→    plan_data: dict | None,
    40→    new_count: int = 0,
    41→) -> None:
    42→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    43→        QueueBreakdown,
    44→        format_queue_headline,
    45→    )
    46→
    47→    total = len(items)
    48→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    49→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    50→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    51→
    52→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    58→    plan_ordered = 0
    59→    if plan_data:
    60→        queue_order = plan_data.get("queue_order", [])
    61→        skipped_ids = set(plan_data.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    62→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    63→
    64→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    65→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    66→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    67→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    68→        subjective=subjective,
    69→    )
    70→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    71→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    72→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    73→
    74→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    75→    if focus:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    77→
    78→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    79→        return
    80→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    81→        return
    82→    print(
    83→        colorize(
    84→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    85→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    92→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    93→        return items[:top]
    94→    return items
    95→
    96→
    97→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    98→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    99→    _new = new_ids or set()
   100→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   101→        pos = str(idx)
   102→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   103→
   104→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   105→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   106→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   107→            conf_str = "—"
   108→            detector = "planning"
   109→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   110→            cluster_name = ""
   111→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   112→            conf_str = "—"
   113→            detector = "workflow"
   114→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   115→            cluster_name = ""
   116→        elif kind == "cluster":
   117→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   118→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   119→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   120→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   121→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   122→                if m.get("id") in _new
   123→            )
   124→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   125→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   126→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   127→        else:
   128→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   129→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   130→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   131→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   132→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   133→
   134→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   135→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   136→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   137→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   138→    return rows
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   143→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   144→    state = runtime.state
   145→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   149→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   150→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   151→
   152→    plan = load_plan()
   153→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   154→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   155→
   156→    queue = build_work_queue(
   157→        state,
   158→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   159→            count=None,
   160→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   161→            status="open",
   162→            include_subjective=True,
   163→            plan=plan_data,
   164→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   165→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   166→            collapse_clusters=True,
   167→        ),
   168→    )
   169→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   170→
   171→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   172→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   173→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last triage)
   174→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   175→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   176→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   177→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   178→
   179→    if sort_by == "recent":
   180→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   181→
   182→    _print_queue_header(
   183→        items=items,
   184→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   185→        plan=plan,
   186→        plan_data=plan_data,
   187→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   188→    )
   189→
   190→    if not items:
   191→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Determine which items to show
   195→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   196→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   197→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   198→
   199→    print()
   200→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   201→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   202→
   203→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   204→        remaining = len(items) - top
   205→        print(colorize(
   206→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   207→        ))
   208→    print()
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   212→
```

```
    16→
    17→def _finding(

    20→    detector: str = "smells",
    21→    file: str = "src/a.py",

    33→        "summary": fid,
    34→        "status": status,
    35→        "detail": detail or {},

    39→def _state(findings: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:

   384→def test_subjective_threshold_clamped_to_valid_range():
   385→    """Threshold values outside [0, 100] are clamped, not rejected."""
   386→    state = _state(
   387→        [],
   388→        dimension_scores={
   389→            "Naming quality": {"score": 50.0, "strict": 50.0, "issues": 1},
   390→        },

   393→    queue = build_work_queue(
   394→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=-10

   397→    assert subj_items == []

   402→    )

   404→    assert len(subj_items2) >= 1

   641→# ── Impact-based ordering ──────────────────────────────────
   642→
   643→
   644→def test_high_impact_subjective_before_low_impact_mechanical():
   645→    """A subjective dimension with high headroom sorts before a mechanical
   646→    finding in a near-perfect dimension."""
   647→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import enrich_with_impact, item_sort_key
   648→
   649→    subj_item = {
   650→        "id": "subjective::naming",
   651→        "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   652→        "detail": {"dimension_name": "Naming quality", "strict_score": 43.0},
   653→        "confidence": "medium",
   654→    }
   655→    mech_item = {
   656→        "id": "smells::src/a.py::x",
   657→        "kind": "finding",
   658→        "detector": "smells",
   659→        "confidence": "high",
   660→        "detail": {"count": 1},
   661→    }
   662→
   663→    dimension_scores = {
   664→        "Naming quality": {
   665→            "score": 43.0,
   666→            "strict": 43.0,
   667→            "issues": 3,
   668→            "detectors": {
   669→                "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   670→            },
   671→        },
   672→        "Code quality": {
   673→            "score": 97.0,
   674→            "strict": 97.0,
   675→            "checks": 200,
   676→            "issues": 1,
   677→            "detectors": {
   678→                "smells": {"weighted_failures": 1, "potential": 200},
   679→            },
   680→        },
   681→    }
   682→
   683→    enrich_with_impact([subj_item, mech_item], dimension_scores)
   684→
   685→    # Naming quality has much more headroom (57) than Code quality (3)
   686→    assert subj_item["estimated_impact"] > mech_item["estimated_impact"]
   687→    assert item_sort_key(subj_item) < item_sort_key(mech_item)
   688→
   689→
   690→def test_impact_fallback_when_no_dimension_scores():
   691→    """When no dimension_scores exist, all items get impact 0.0 and
   692→    tiebreakers provide default ordering."""
   693→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import enrich_with_impact, item_sort_key
   694→
   695→    items = [
   696→        {
   697→            "id": "smells::src/a.py::x",
   698→            "kind": "finding",
   699→            "detector": "smells",
   700→            "confidence": "high",
   701→            "detail": {"count": 5},
   702→        },
   703→        {
   704→            "id": "smells::src/b.py::y",
   705→            "kind": "finding",
   706→            "detector": "smells",
   707→            "confidence": "medium",
   708→            "detail": {"count": 2},
   709→        },
   710→    ]
   711→
   712→    enrich_with_impact(items, {})
   713→
   714→    assert all(item["estimated_impact"] == 0.0 for item in items)
   715→    # High confidence sorts before medium when impact is equal
   716→    assert item_sort_key(items[0]) < item_sort_key(items[1])

   835→# ── Cluster collapse ─────────────────────────────────────
   836→
   837→
   838→def test_collapse_clusters_preserves_order():
   839→    """Cluster meta-item appears at position of first member, not re-sorted."""
   840→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
   841→
   842→    plan: dict = {
   843→        "queue_order": [],
   844→        "skipped": {},
   845→        "overrides": {},
   846→        "clusters": {},
   847→        "active_cluster": None,
   848→    }
   849→    plan["clusters"]["auto/unused"] = {
   850→        "name": "auto/unused",
   851→        "auto": True,
   852→        "cluster_key": "auto::unused",
   853→        "finding_ids": ["u1", "u2"],
   854→        "description": "Remove 2 unused findings",
   855→        "action": "desloppify autofix unused-imports --dry-run",
   856→        "user_modified": False,
   857→    }
   858→
   859→    # Place a non-cluster item first, then the two cluster members
   860→    items = [
   861→        {"id": "other", "kind": "finding", "detector": "structural",
   862→         "confidence": "medium", "detail": {}},
   863→        {"id": "u1", "kind": "finding", "detector": "unused",
   864→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
   865→        {"id": "u2", "kind": "finding", "detector": "unused",
   866→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
   867→    ]
   868→
   869→    result = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   870→    # Non-cluster item stays at position 0
   871→    assert result[0]["id"] == "other"
   872→    # Cluster meta-item appears at position 1 (where first member was)
   873→    assert result[1]["kind"] == "cluster"
   874→    assert result[1]["id"] == "auto/unused"
   875→    assert len(result) == 2  # other + cluster
   876→

   878→# -- Plan-ordered subjective items surface despite objective backlog --------
   879→
   880→
   881→def test_plan_ordered_stale_subjective_surfaces_with_objective_backlog():
   882→    """Stale subjective items in plan queue_order surface even with objective backlog.
   883→
   884→    After a completed cycle, stale dims are injected into queue_order by
   885→    sync_stale_dimensions.  The work queue must respect the plan's explicit
   886→    ordering and surface them even though should_surface() would normally
   887→    filter them.
   888→    """
   889→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   890→
   891→    objective_findings = [
   892→        _finding(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   893→        for c in "abcd"
   894→    ]
   895→    state = _state(
   896→        objective_findings,
   897→        dimension_scores={
   898→            "Naming quality": {
   899→                "score": 70.0,
   900→                "strict": 70.0,
   901→                "issues": 1,
   902→                "detectors": {
   903→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   904→                },
   905→            },
   906→        },
   907→    )
   908→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   909→        "naming_quality": {
   910→            "score": 70.0,
   911→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   912→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   913→        }
   914→    }
   915→
   916→    # Without plan: stale subjective item is gated (existing behavior)
   917→    queue_no_plan = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   918→    subj_no_plan = [
   919→        i["id"] for i in queue_no_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   920→    ]
   921→    assert len(subj_no_plan) == 0
   922→
   923→    # With plan that includes the stale dim in queue_order: surfaces
   924→    plan = empty_plan()
   925→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   926→        "subjective::naming_quality",
   927→        "smells::src/a.py::x",
   928→        "smells::src/b.py::x",
   929→        "smells::src/c.py::x",
   930→        "smells::src/d.py::x",
   931→    ]
   932→    queue_with_plan = build_work_queue(
   933→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan,
   934→    )
   935→    subj_with_plan = [
   936→        i["id"] for i in queue_with_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   937→    ]
   938→    assert len(subj_with_plan) == 1
   939→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in subj_with_plan
   940→
   941→    # And it should be first in the queue (plan order respected)
   942→    assert queue_with_plan["items"][0]["id"] == "subjective::naming_quality"
   943→
```

```
1→"""Finding prioritization/selection helpers for next items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanItem, PlanState
     6→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def get_next_items(
    13→    state: PlanState,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int = 1,
    16→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    17→) -> list[PlanItem]:
    18→    """Get the N highest-priority open findings.
    19→
    20→    Legacy plan API intentionally returns only finding items (not synthetic
    21→    subjective queue items) so existing planner consumers stay stable.
    22→    The *tier* parameter is accepted for backward compatibility but ignored.
    23→    """
    24→    result = build_work_queue(
    25→        state,
    26→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    27→            count=count,
    28→            scan_path=scan_path,
    29→            status="open",
    30→            include_subjective=False,
    31→        ),
    32→    )
    33→    return [item for item in result["items"] if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    34→
    35→
    36→def get_next_item(
    37→    state: PlanState,
    38→    tier: int | None = None,
    39→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    40→) -> PlanItem | None:
    41→    """Get the highest-priority open finding."""
    42→    items = get_next_items(state, tier=tier, count=1, scan_path=scan_path)
    43→    return items[0] if items else None
    44→
```

```
   246→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   247→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   248→
   249→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   250→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   251→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   252→    """
   253→    findings = state["findings"]
   254→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   255→
   256→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   257→    if plan is None:
   258→        try:
   259→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   260→            plan = load_plan()
   261→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   262→            plan = {}
   263→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   264→        plan = {}
   265→    plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   266→    plan.setdefault("skipped", {})
   267→    plan.setdefault("clusters", {})
   268→
   269→    has_plan = bool(
   270→        plan
   271→        and (
   272→            plan.get("queue_order")
   273→            or plan.get("skipped")
   274→            or plan.get("clusters")
   275→        )
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   279→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   280→    lines.extend(_summary_lines(stats))
   281→
   282→    if has_plan:
   283→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   284→        queue = build_work_queue(
   285→            state,
   286→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   287→                count=None,
   288→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   289→                status="open",
   290→                include_subjective=True,
   291→            ),
   292→        )
   293→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   294→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   295→
   296→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   297→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   298→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   299→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   300→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   301→        if remaining:
   302→            lines.append("---")
   303→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   304→            lines.append("")
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import is_subjective_queue_item
    10→
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class QueueBreakdown:
    18→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    19→
    20→    queue_total: int = 0
    21→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    22→    skipped: int = 0
    23→    subjective: int = 0
    24→    workflow: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→
    30→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    plan: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    34→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    35→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    36→
    37→    result = build_work_queue(
    38→        state,
    39→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    40→            status="open",
    41→            count=None,
    42→            plan=plan,
    43→            collapse_clusters=True,
    44→            include_skipped=False,
    45→        ),
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    queue_total = result["total"]
    49→
    50→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
    51→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
    52→    items = result.get("items", [])
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for item in items
    55→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    56→    )
    57→    workflow = sum(
    58→        1 for item in items
    59→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    # Plan-derived counts
    63→    plan_ordered = 0
    64→    skipped = 0
    65→    if plan:
    66→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    67→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    68→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    71→
    72→    # Focus cluster info
    73→    focus_cluster = None
    74→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    75→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    76→    if plan:
    77→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→        if active:
    79→            focus_cluster = active
    80→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    81→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    83→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    84→            open_findings = {
    85→                fid
    86→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    87→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    88→            }
    89→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    90→
    91→    return QueueBreakdown(
    92→        queue_total=queue_total,
    93→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    94→        skipped=skipped,
    95→        subjective=subjective,
    96→        workflow=workflow,
    97→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    98→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
    99→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→
   107→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   108→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   109→
   110→    Examples::
   111→
   112→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   113→        Queue: 1934 items
   114→    """
   115→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   116→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   117→
   118→    # Parenthesized segments
   119→    segments: list[str] = []
   120→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   121→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   122→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   123→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   124→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   125→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   126→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   127→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   128→    if segments:
   129→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   130→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   131→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   132→    return label
   133→
   134→
   135→def format_queue_block(
   136→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   137→    *,
   138→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   139→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   140→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   141→
   142→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   143→    """
   144→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   145→
   146→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   147→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   148→        focus_line = (
   149→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   150→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   151→            f" items remaining"
   152→        )
   153→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   154→
   155→    # Frozen score line
   156→    if frozen_score is not None:
   157→        lines.append((
   158→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   159→            "cyan",
   160→        ))
   161→
   162→    # Queue headline — always the same
   163→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   164→
   165→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   166→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   167→        lines.append((
   168→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   169→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   170→            "dim",
   171→        ))
   172→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   173→        lines.append((
   174→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   175→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        ))
   178→    else:
   179→        lines.append((
   180→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   181→            "dim",
   182→        ))
   183→
   184→    return lines
   185→
   186→
   187→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   188→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   189→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   190→
   191→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   192→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
   193→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
   194→
   195→    result = build_work_queue(
   196→        state,
   197→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   198→            status="open",
   199→            count=None,
   200→            plan=plan,
   201→            collapse_clusters=True,
   202→            include_skipped=False,
   203→        ),
   204→    )
   205→    return result["total"]
   206→
   207→
   208→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   209→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   210→    if not plan:
   211→        return None
   212→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   213→
   214→
   215→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   216→    plan: dict,
   217→    queue_remaining: int,
   218→    *,
   219→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   222→
   223→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   224→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   225→    """
   226→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   227→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   228→    if strict is None:
   229→        return
   230→
   231→    if breakdown is not None:
   232→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   233→        print()
   234→        for text, style in block:
   235→            print(colorize(text, style))
   236→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   237→            print(colorize(
   238→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   239→                "dim",
   240→            ))
   241→        return
   242→
   243→    # Legacy fallback
   244→    print(
   245→        colorize(
   246→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   247→            "cyan",
   248→        )
   249→    )
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   253→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   254→            "dim",
   255→        )
   256→    )
   257→
   258→
   259→def _print_objective_drained_banner(
   260→    frozen_strict: float,
   261→    remaining: int,
   262→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   263→) -> None:
   264→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   265→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   266→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   267→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   268→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   269→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   270→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   271→    print(colorize(
   272→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   273→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   274→        "cyan",
   275→    ))
   276→    print(colorize(
   277→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   278→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   279→        "dim",
   280→    ))
   281→
   282→
   283→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   284→    state: dict,
   285→    prev,
   286→    plan: dict | None,
   287→) -> None:
   288→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   289→
   290→    Three display modes:
   291→
   292→    1. **Frozen** — objective queue items remain → show frozen plan-start score.
   293→    2. **Phase transition** — objective drained, subjective/workflow remains
   294→       → show live scores + transition banner.
   295→    3. **Live** — no active plan cycle or queue is clear → show live scores.
   296→    """
   297→    frozen_strict: float | None = None
   298→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   299→
   300→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   301→        frozen_strict = plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]
   302→        try:
   303→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   304→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   305→            pass
   306→
   307→    remaining = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   308→
   309→    # Frozen: objective work remains — show frozen plan-start score and return
   310→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   311→        objective_remaining = remaining - breakdown.subjective - breakdown.workflow
   312→        if objective_remaining > 0:
   313→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   314→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   315→            )
   316→            return
   317→
   318→    # Live scores (or phase transition): show current scores
   319→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   320→
   321→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   322→
   323→    # Phase transition: objective drained but subjective/workflow remains
   324→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   325→        _print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, remaining, breakdown)
   326→
   327→
   328→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   329→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   330→
   331→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   332→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   333→    """
   334→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   335→
   336→    try:
   337→        plan = load_plan()
   338→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   339→        plan = None
   340→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   341→
   342→
   343→__all__ = [
   344→    "QueueBreakdown",
   345→    "format_queue_block",
   346→    "format_queue_headline",
   347→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   348→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   349→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   350→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   351→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   352→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   353→]
   354→
```
